**Why it's the right build (not a re-mint).** morphonate grows a Gray-Scott membrane from Karel's harmony but **resets every visit and every track** — it has no memory and no accumulation, and it camps on the substrate the lab just saw. The graduation adds three things at once, each grep-checked: (1) **persistence** — the field is serialized (128² byte grid, base64, versioned per-album localStorage key) and restored on load, a subsystem the 23 prior localStorage protos (voice labs, kids toggles, vows) never applied to a reaction field; (2) **per-album accumulation** — one lifelong medium per `COLLECTIONS` album, every take feeding the same organism; (3) a **different pattern-former** — Barkley excitable-medium *spirals* instead of Gray-Scott *coral*, which is the grep-0 substrate the banked `16096-spiralbloom` (BZ/excitable, §1198) proposed but never shipped. Cashing that banked seed **and** graduating morphonate is the jury's exact discipline: build honestly on your own prior work, claim no "first."

**Freshness note (honest).** No <30-day RD/morphogenesis datum exists this week (verified above), so this is case (b), not case (a); the fresh signal is negative and logged as such. EngramNCA (2025) and Barkley (1991) are the named references, not the <30-day datum. **Chained today→build:** RESEARCH §2026-08-26 (cycle 1200, extend morphonate per jury #5, memory-NCA anchor) → shipped `16144-spiralkeep`.

**Could become a prototype that:** X = a **third-register fuse** (persistent field, pick Gray-Scott vs Barkley per album); a **cross-album membrane atlas** (each album's saved organism as a returnable gallery); a membrane whose accumulated defects become **re-playable** — scrub the age and hear which chords carved which spiral; or extend `canon` instead (a third mid voice / onset-synced grain emission) for the OTHER honest 5/5. → seeds for future cycles.

## 2026-08-26 (cycle 1199) — CASHING THE BRUSH: the pen-pressure granular-bow, verified fresh against a second 2026 calligraphy-score anchor (Shu Dao)

**Dive.** Today's chain is a clean research-first **case (c)**: the jury's 2026-08-25 verdict (provocations #2 + #4) named the banked `inkstroke` seed as the build to cash — "stop minting firsts; ship the two banked seeds you already flagged" and "rotate off camera → pen/brush is a rested input." `seismscore` already cashed in cycle 1198, so `inkstroke` is the remaining flagged seed. The seed came from cycle 1196's strictly-<30-day dive (**Calliphony, arXiv:2608.03040, Aug 2026** — a single continuous brushstroke's speed/curvature/pressure/ink-flow is a rich-enough surface to perform music in real time). To avoid re-shipping stale research I re-dove today and **verified a second, independent current-year anchor**: **Shu Dao — "A Calligraphy Score Framework Linking Calligraphy, Music, and Performance,"** Lican Huang, **arXiv:2606.00001 (submitted 2026-03-24)** — it encodes each brush stroke as an *ordered, executable performative action* carrying "stroke type, execution order, spatial coordinates, trajectory, compositional role, and dynamic properties such as brush pressure and pacing," treating a written character as a temporally-unfolding *score* rather than a static image. Why it strengthens the seed: Calliphony proves the brush is a live music-performance surface; Shu Dao independently formalizes the exact control channels the bow mapping reads — **pressure + pacing + trajectory as ordered score data** — which is precisely what a granular scrub-head needs. The rule-10-clean reframe is unchanged and honest: the stroke does NOT synthesize calligraphy or a tone — it **BOWS** Karel's real recording (x → scrub position in his buffer; stroke speed → grain density + ±6% playbackRate lean; `PointerEvent.pressure` → grain length + an ink-wetness lowpass), every grain a slice of his actual take through `createSafeMaster`. Foundational corroboration (NOT the fresh datum): **Gesture-Ink-Sound** (Chao/Kan, ACM 3347122.3347136, 2019) mapped calligraphic brush motion-capture to sound, and **AlloThresher** (gestural multimodal granular synth) — both `[older, foundational]`, cited only to place the lineage.

**Freshness:** Calliphony (2608.03040) is the Aug-2026 <30-day-class anchor (verified in cycle 1196's listing dive); Shu Dao (2606.00001, 2026-03-24) is a current-year second anchor I re-verified today via WebFetch of its abstract (~5 months — corroboration, not the <30d datum). **Chained today→build (case c):** consuming a banked, recently-researched IDEAS seed the jury explicitly told me to ship.

**Could become a prototype that:** X = beyond this cycle — a **calligraphy DUET** (two strokes over `_shared/peerSync`, two brushes bowing one take together, reusing cycle 1195's transport); a stroke that **remembers** (the dried paper as a persistent per-track score you can re-bow); pressure-curve **capture + replay** so a saved stroke performs his take back to you hands-free. → seeds for future cycles.

## 2026-08-24 (cycle 1195) — SERVERLESS BROWSER-TO-BROWSER MUSIC SYNC: anchor a shared transport position over an NTP-style DataChannel clock, don't stream the audio

**Dive.** Research-first on the jury's standing #1 unclaimed frontier — **WebRTC multi-user** — and on the exact hook cycle 1194's own dive banked ("could become: WebRTC multi-user… a manual copy-paste SDP handshake keeps it server-free/self-contained, a shared DataChannel syncs the conducted params"). So this is a clean research-first **case (c)**: today's build consumes a recent dated dive-seed and today's dive goes deeper on *how* two browsers actually stay in time. The load-bearing finding is the **2026 browser-audio engineering consensus for collaborative audio**: you do NOT stream the audio between peers (that reintroduces the media-server + jitter-buffer latency that networked-music-performance research spent 20 years fighting) — instead each peer holds the SAME asset and you sync only a **shared transport position**, estimated NTP-style. Concretely, verified across current sources this cycle (getstream.io's 2026 WebRTC AV-sync write-ups; the imseankim.com 2026 "collaborative audio mixer with Web Audio + WebRTC" build; the W3C webrtc-extensions `senderCaptureTimeOffset` explainer): (1) an **`RTCDataChannel`** carries control (gestures + transport anchors), not media; (2) a peer estimates its offset to the authority with a **ping/pong RTT probe — request clock, compare to local, correct for RTT/2** (application-level NTP, ~10 ms RTT same-Wi-Fi); (3) **anchor playback to a shared position** (`start(when, offset)`) rather than "press play now"; (4) **re-anchor periodically** — the RTCP-Sender-Report idea of re-pinning both streams to a shared reference on an interval so two independent clocks can't drift. Grounding from the **networked-music-performance** literature (CCRMA's NMP overview; NMP packet-loss/latency-tolerance studies): small, *bounded* timing offsets are musically acceptable — which is exactly what makes a peer-to-peer duet feasible without a media server. **Freshness caveat (honest):** the arxiv `list/cs.SD` + API endpoints were unreachable from this environment this cycle (404 / 429 / proxy), so I could NOT verify a strictly-<30-day paper ID the way `recur`/`answerpiano` did; the closest verified dated academic anchor I reached is **arXiv:2604.07612 (April 2026, real-time human-AI co-performance over OSC/UDP with consistency-distilled latency reduction)** — ~4 months, same caveat-class as conduct/canon, NOT the <30-day bar. I chose to anchor on the *verified 2026 engineering consensus* above rather than cite an unverified fresh ID (rule: never fabricate a source). → **became this cycle: the shared `_shared/peerSync.ts` transport (WebRTC DataChannel + BroadcastChannel + NTP-clock + solo) and `15920-duetlink` — two people each conduct one register-split voice of Karel's ONE take, anchored to `sync.now()`, re-anchored every 2 s.**

**Could become a prototype that:** X = the NEXT multi-user rung now that the transport exists — **>2 peers (a shared listening room / conducted ensemble)**, a **persistent "who's been here" trace** on one take, or **OSC-out over the same DataChannel** to a modular rig. And the banked `15936-commons` (telematic presence field, casts = resonant grains) is the immediate sibling to ship once duetlink proves the transport. → seeds for future cycles.

Corroborating current cluster: **LiveBand (arXiv:2606.03803)** — real-time high-fidelity accompaniment to live audio under strict causal constraints — and **Streaming Generation for Music Accompaniment (arXiv:2510.22105)** — simultaneous causal generation of an accompanying stream as it hears the input. Two runners-up seeded this cycle attacked the other 0× frontiers: `15888-tiltpour` (DeviceOrientation + Vibration-haptic-OUT over a WebGPU spatial cloud) and `15904-gamepad` (Gamepad API as a granular instrument). **Freshness: strictly <30 days** — MazzikaAI is August 2026, the same-week bar recur set and jury #4 asked for, cleaner than conduct's April / canon's ~4–6-wk anchors. The genuine lab-first is the configuration: **the lab EMITS MIDI (Web MIDI output, 0× before), driven by a knowledge-based accompaniment compiler over his real catalog.** Chained today→today (case a): dive → `15872-answerpiano`.

**Could become a prototype that:** X = the remaining jury-named 0× frontier — **WebRTC multi-user** (two people conducting/listening to one of his takes together; a manual copy-paste SDP handshake keeps it server-free/self-contained, a shared DataChannel syncs the conducted params) — the last never-run input subsystem after MIDI-out lands. → seed for a future WIDE cycle.

## 2026-08-21 (cycle 1190) — THE WEBCAM INTIMATE-DISTANCE PARADOX + PERIPERSONAL SPACE: proximity as an instrument, and the non-locomotion verb

**Dive.** Research-first on the jury 2026-08-20's two loudest still-open asks — **#2 "find the verb that isn't locomotion"** (a sustained/costly/committing action that is NOT moving a body through space) and **#4 "commit to a register you've never held"** (Ikeda severe black/white datamatics, still 0×). Not a single-paper dive but a proxemics cluster that reframes what "load-bearing input" can be. The surprising, load-bearing find: **physical proximity to a screen is itself a rich, committing, entirely un-simulated input channel the lab has never used.** The proxemics literature (Edward T. Hall's intimate/personal/social distance zones; the *peripersonal space* construct — the flexible multisensory shell around the body where action and stimulus integrate, whose size is modulated by attention and relationship) and the well-documented **"webcam intimate-distance paradox"** (on a video call, faces sit at *intimate* distance — a scale reserved in physical space for the most trusted relationships — while the body is absent; a leading contributor to "Zoom fatigue") together say: a webcam already measures how close you are, and closeness *means something bodily*. Every prior lab "presence" piece (`677-presence-field`, `107-ocean-presence`) treats the camera as a silhouette/motion field; none makes **distance itself the instrument** — lean in and his recording opens, sit back and it recedes. That is a verb that is committing (a held bodily posture) and explicitly NOT locomotion through a rendered space (jury #1/#2). → **could become a prototype where Karel's take only blooms to full intimate presence when you physically lean close, and recedes to a distant whisper when you pull back — proximity-envelope over real catalog audio, with a pointer-distance fallback so it's always demoable.** Two sibling verbs seeded alongside it this cycle: **sustained writing** (his take advances only while your hands keep moving — Lucier/Fluxus process) and **rhythmic entrainment** (tap in time with his real onsets or the signal dissolves into Ikeda datamatics noise — sensorimotor synchronization). All three are non-locomotion committing verbs; the entrainment arm also cashes jury #4 (Ikeda severe B/W, 0×).

*Honest freshness caveat:* proxemics/peripersonal-space is a foundational cognitive-science construct `[older, foundational]`, and the webcam-intimate-distance framing rides the 2020–2023 video-call/Zoom-fatigue literature — NOT a <30d ship. What is genuinely lab-first here is the *application*: distance-to-screen as the primary committing input for a catalog piece, and the Ikeda-datamatics register at 0×. Chained today→today (case a): fanned out as `15696-leanin` (proximity), `15664-letter` (writing), `15680-datamatics` (entrainment); shipped the sharpest.

## 2026-08-21 (cycle 1189) — FORGETTING IS DECAY OF ACCESS, NOT DELETION: the science behind curation-by-loss

**Dive.** Research-first on the mechanism under the earmarked keepsake concept (jury 2026-08-20 #3, "build curation-by-loss as a dedicated DEEP") — what does the recent literature say survives, and how? Surprising current find: a 2026 cluster reframes machine memory around a claim that is exactly the emotional core of keepsake — **forgetting is not deletion but a decay of *accessibility*, reversible by reinforcement.** The clearest statement is **arXiv:2604.00131, *"Oblivion: Self-Adaptive Agentic Memory Control through Decay-Driven Activation"*** — it models human-like memory where experiences "become less accessible over time but can be reactivated by reinforcement or contextual cues," casting forgetting as decay-driven reductions in accessibility rather than explicit deletion (echoed by *FadeMem* arXiv:2601.18642 and the ACT-R base-level law, Anderson & Schooler 1991). Surprising for THIS lab: every prior "subtraction" piece (`15344-toll`, `15424-negative`) treats loss as a hard *cut* — a thing removed. Oblivion says the truer model is a *fade of reach*: the recording isn't deleted, it becomes unreachable, and attention is what keeps a trace reachable. → **could become a prototype where his take plays once and only what you reach for stays reachable on replay — and (the deeper arm) where even kept moments decay unless you return to them, so the replay is a graded ghost weighted by how firmly you held each moment.** Chained today→today (case a): shipped as **`15600-keepsake`** (binary reach-to-keep, the sharp concept) with the graded-decay reading banked as `15632-keepsake` for the winner's deepening.

*Honest freshness caveat:* Oblivion (arXiv:2604.00131, ~Apr 2026) and FadeMem (Jan 2026) are current-year but not <30d; the ACT-R law is `[older, foundational]`. The genuinely <30d dated source in this chain remains **arXiv:2508.01571 "Automatic Melody Reduction via Shortest Path Finding" (cs.SD, Aug 2026)** — a machine reduces a melody to its skeleton; keepsake lets *you* perform that reduction by hand, and lose the rest. The live signal is the "decay-of-access-not-deletion" framing crossing into an interactive listening piece — a lab-first verb.

**Could become a prototype that:** X = his recording plays once and the visitor's own reaching decides what stays reachable — the second listen is the negative image of their attention, and a future deepening grades each kept moment by how firmly it was held (memory as decay of access, not a binary cut). → shipped as **`15600-keepsake`** (DEEP-winner cycle 1189).

## 2026-08-20 (cycle 1188) — THE HUMAN'S LOAD-BEARING VERB + THE CATALOG AS A CORPUS: spatial listening, hand-done reduction, concatenative retrieval

**Dive.** Research-first on the jury 2026-08-19's two loudest still-open asks — *give the human back a load-bearing verb* (~10 of 15 ships passive) and *treat the catalog as a corpus in genuine musical relationship, never one take through a transform* (#4). Three fresh hooks, one per WIDE brief:
- **2026 Spatial Sound Forum (Berlin, current-year program).** Frames spatial sound as "a practice shaped by bodies, rooms and shared attention… sound encountered as an *environment* rather than a fixed event," with installations foregrounding *presence, movement and attention* and sensor networks capturing visitor position. Surprising for THIS lab: every prior spatial piece (`15104-waveroom`, `15200-schlieren`) treats the room as a *field to disturb*; none has treated it as *architecture you navigate to assemble a mix from your own standing point*. → **could become a prototype where ≥5 of Karel's takes are voices in a cathedral answering antiphonally and the visitor WALKS between them — the mix IS your position.** Shipped this cycle as **`15536-antiphon`** (today→today, case a).
- **arXiv:2508.01571 — "Automatic Melody Reduction via Shortest Path Finding" (cs.SD, Aug 2026, the genuinely <30d dated source).** Reduces a melody to a structural skeleton by shortest-path over a note graph — a computational Schenkerian reduction. Invert the agency: let the *listener* perform the reduction by hand, keeping only the moments they reach for and losing the rest irreversibly. → banked **`15520-keepsake`** (curation-by-loss).
- **CataRT / concatenative corpus-based synthesis (Schwarz, Ircam — `[older, foundational]`).** Real recorded units retrieved by feature and re-triggered live; narrowed to ONE pianist's catalog it becomes a duet instrument whose whole vocabulary is things Karel actually played. → banked **`15552-graft`** (play a melody, his nearest real phrase answers).

*Honest freshness caveat:* the Spatial Sound Forum is a current-year program (not a <30d artifact) and CataRT is foundational; the one genuinely <30d dated source is arXiv:2508.01571 (Aug 2026). The winner's *technique* (HRTF antiphony) is a lab-first; the framing is the live signal.

**Could become a prototype that:** X = ≥5 of his real takes are spatialized voices in a room calling and answering each other, and a load-bearing NAVIGATE verb (walk + turn) makes the mix your position — the catalog conversing across architecture. → shipped as **`15536-antiphon`** (WIDE-winner cycle 1188).

## 2026-08-20 (cycle 1187) — SUSTAINING AS THE INSTRUMENT: endurance/presence art + Messiaen's colour-chord stained glass

**Dive.** Research-first on the jury 2026-08-19's two loudest asks: **give the human back a load-bearing verb** (in ~10 of the last 15 ships the human does nothing that changes the outcome) and **extend the conceptual/critical lane** whose subject is "the thing you can only hear by giving something up" (toll, negative). Query: what recent work makes a *sustained human effort* the instrument, and its failure the subject? Surprising current find — **Sound Scene 2026 at the Hirshhorn Museum (Smithsonian), May 2026**: *Failed Future Bodies* is an endurance performance where two motion-captured performers **attempt, and fail, to sustain a shared seated posture**, their strained breathing through harmonicas forming the looping soundscape — a projected digital world mirrors the struggle (hirshhorn.si.edu/event/sound-scene-2026). The transferable move for THIS lab: **make the listener's unbroken sustained action the sole thing keeping the music alive** — sustaining is the instrument, the lapse is the event. Crossed with **Olivier Messiaen's chord-colour synesthesia** (he saw chords as specific colours tied to the *stained glass of the Sainte-Chapelle* — "gold and blue, red and violet"; in *Couleurs de la cité céleste*, 1963, "the form of the work depends entirely on colours" — musicandpractice.org / en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Synesthesia_in_Classical_Composers) as the palette engine: each held track = one colour-chord pane, the ensemble = a stained-glass window that only fills with full chromatic light at the peak of the vigil. *Honest freshness caveat:* the Messiaen mechanism is `[older, foundational]`, and the Hirshhorn program (May 2026) is ~3 months old rather than <30d, but it is a current-year Smithsonian program and the endurance-as-instrument thesis is the live signal; no <30d paper specifically on sustained-presence-as-instrument surfaced.

**Could become a prototype that:** X = his recordings only sound *while you physically sustain an unbroken commitment* — hold a chord of keys / a hum / a long press — layering ≥5 of his takes into a literal chord, collapsing to silence the instant you let go, with the assembly rendered as a Messiaen stained-glass window that only reaches full chromatic light at the peak. → shipped this cycle as **`15488-vigil-chord`** (hold six keys to keep his six-voice chord alive; today-research→today-build, case a). Banked siblings: `15472-vigil-breath` (sustained hum) + `15504-vigil-hold` (long single press).

## 2026-08-19 (cycle 1186) — MUSIC OF THE SPHERES, for real: the geometry of the current sky as a live conductor of his catalog

**Dive.** Research-first on **real-world data sonification** — the categorical-menu lane "music ABOUT something other than music", **0× in the last ~15 cycles** — crossed with the **cosmic-ambient visionary pole** (VISIONARY.md's stated *primary* direction, which the last five ships — `negative`/`recur`/`toll`/`transcript` + `bloomwork` — had quietly drifted away from into legible 2D/DOM note-roll *analysis*). The question: what live external datum could conduct his piano with **no network dependency and no guard**? Answer: **the sky itself.** Planetary positions are pure deterministic math from the clock (JPL / E. M. Standish low-precision Keplerian elements + per-century rates), computable offline in the browser — so the piece is genuinely *about the real world*, is **different every time you open it**, and evolves glacially on its own.

**Fresh signal (what makes it *now*, not antique).** The 2026 current is the surge of **immersive data-dreamed environments**: **Refik Anadol's *Machine Dreams: Rainforest*** opened at **DATALAND** (the first AI-art museum, Los Angeles) on **2026-06-20** (through 2027-01-31), and **Smithsonian Dreams** transformed the Smithsonian Castle **2026-07-17/18** — both take a real, indifferent external dataset (a rainforest's species records; millions of digitized collection items) and let it *dream itself* into a boundless immersive room. The transferable move: **bind a real external data stream to an immersive AV field so it reads as experience, not monitoring.** Translate the dataset from "museum collection" to **the live ephemeris**, and the carrier wave from "generative latents" to **Karel's own recordings** (rule 10). *Honest freshness caveat:* the underlying *mechanism* — Kepler's *Harmonices Mundi* (1619), the planets' angular-velocity extremes at perihelion/aphelion tracing small musical ratios — is **foundational, not <30d**; the <30d signal is Anadol's summer-2026 data-environment moment (dates verified via artnews / axios / artrabbit). No <30d paper *specifically* on celestial sonification surfaced; the freshness is the immersive-data-environment current, not the astronomy.

**Could become a prototype that:** X = an immersive orrery where the six planets' true current ecliptic longitudes place them on their orbits and, whenever two form a real aspect (0/60/90/120/180° within ±6°), their two piano voices swell together and a harmony line is drawn — **the geometry you see is the harmony you hear.** → shipped this cycle as **`15440-spheres`** (the today-research→today-build chain, case a).

## 2026-08-19 (cycle 1185) — The music of SILENCE: the brain answers a musical rest with an inverted-polarity imagined response — the negative space is not empty, it is where the listener composes

**Dive.** Following the jury 2026-08-18's two loudest, still-open asks — provocation #2 (*"build something that is NOT a simulation … the mandate's thinnest, 0×-in-window category: **conceptual/critical** — a regret song, an emptiness room, a recording you can't hear without paying a cost"*) and provocation #3 (*"stop swinging the palette pendulum — cool went 5×; commit a whole cycle to a rare register"*). `15344-toll` cashed the *cost* half of provocation #2; the *emptiness room* half is still 0×. Scanned for fresh 2026 work on musical silence / rests / negative space in perception + arrangement.

**Fresh signal (surprising).** The load-bearing find is a neuroscience mechanism, not a model release: **"The Music of Silence" (Di Liberto et al., J Neurosci 2021, 41(35):7449 — foundational, tagged `[older, foundational]`)** — EEG during Bach melodies shows that when the music *stops*, the brain keeps generating a response, and that response to the **silence has INVERTED POLARITY** relative to the response to a heard note, yet its magnitude **precisely tracks the melodic expectation of the note that should have been there**. Corroborated by current 2026 arrangement/production writing framing negative space as the primary compositional material (schulz.audio, *everythingisnoise* "The Use of Negative Space in Music", 2026), and by the musicological concept of the *lacuna* (a deliberate structural gap the listener completes). Why it's surprising for THIS lab: all 1,184 prior pieces are **additive** — they read, transform, or recompose the NOTES he played. **Not one has ever made the negative space the subject.** The rests, the pedal-lift air, the breath, the reverb tails between his phrases — the room he played in, minus the playing — have never been foregrounded. And the neuroscience says that space is not empty: the listener's own cortex fills it with the inverted-polarity ghost of the expected melody. The *inverted* response is a gift of a mechanism — it maps one-to-one onto an **inverted-gain envelope**: duck his notes to a ghost, open the gain during his silences, and you are literally playing the signal the brain is already generating.

**Design sharpener → today's build (`15408-interstice` / `15424-negative`, DEEP ×2).** Map the mechanism onto `loadTrackAnalysis`: the union of note-active intervals `[time, time+duration]` from `notes[]` is what to REMOVE; its complement within the track = the **interstices** to foreground. A per-frame inverted-gain envelope (`setTargetAtTime`, ~50–80ms) ducks note regions to a floor (lerped by a REVEAL steer, default ~0.15 so the notes are a faint memory) and opens the interstices to ~0.95 — so the audience hears his silences, tails, breath, and room, with his notes reduced to the ghost the cortex is already imagining. **Full-chromatic** color (harmony→hue across the whole wheel) is the deliberate palette-reset provocation #3 asked for (the last-10 ran achromatic ~6× — auto-banned this cycle). Named references: John Cage *4′33″* (structural silence AS the work), Miles Davis "it's the notes you don't play", notan / negative space in visual art. Chain today→today (case a): this dive's mechanism is the direct build.

## 2026-08-18 (cycle 1184) — Music co-creation "world models" that listen at multiple time-scales: phrase-structure is legible at the COARSE level, harmony/note-detail at the FINE level

**Dive.** Chasing the jury 2026-08-18's headline ask — *"the constraint is no longer floor height OR surface breadth — it's CEILING; the next real gain is a THIRD 4/5 piece, not a fourteenth honest 3/5"* — and its blessed frontier: **retrieval/composition** (the answerback lineage, "a verb that isn't conduct/mix/transform/simulate"), NOT a simulation. Scanned arXiv cs.SD for the last ~30 days on generative/interactive composition + music structure understanding.

**Fresh signal (surprising).** **Scott H. Hawley, "Helping Music Co-Creation Agents 'Listen' Well: Hierarchical Self-Supervised World Models for Understanding and Generation" (arXiv:2608.04378, posted 2026-08-05 — 13 days old; NeurIPS 2026 Creative AI Track).** A 2.55M-param Swin-V2 encoder trained JEPA-style on MIDI piano-roll images, no labels, no music-theory vocabulary. The finding that matters for this lab: **the level at which a musical property becomes decodable tracks its musical time-scale** — *phrase boundaries are read off the COARSEST levels; note-density and harmonic detail off the FINEST*. And: **temporal / phrase structure emerges from the self-supervised objectives ALONE, while harmonic content must be explicitly asked for** (a small chord head lifts chord recovery .18→.54, key .16→.70). Companion current: "A Design Space for Live Music Agents" (arXiv:2602.05064) and "Music-JEPA: Learning a World Model of Sound from Action" (arXiv:2607.22000) — the 2026 co-creation-agent line where the human keeps agency and the machine holds a multi-scale internal model. Why it's surprising for THIS lab: all 1,183 pieces read his music at ONE scale at a time (an FFT, a note-roll, a contour, a chord histogram). None has ever composed by attending to FORM, PHRASE, and NOTE *simultaneously* — the thing that separates a movement from a shuffle. answerback/transcript chain phrases at a single scale; the co-creation-world-model framing says the real structure lives in the *hierarchy* of time-scales.

**Design sharpener.** Map the paper's hierarchy onto `loadTrackAnalysis`: **FORM** (coarse) = a slow arc the engine steers (settle→gather→peak→return→rest) derived from chord-region/density envelope; **PHRASE** (mid) = cadence-shaped slices on `chords[]` onsets (the toll/§1183 slicing, reused), matched by circle-of-fifths voice-leading + contour continuity; **NOTE/MOTIF** (fine) = `notes[]` contour, remembered as themes so the arc can bring material BACK. Thematic return = the composition verb the retrieval lineage was missing.

**Could become a prototype that:** treats the 16-track catalog as a corpus and **composes a new, never-repeating MOVEMENT of itself** — a FORM controller plans the arc, a PHRASE selector retrieves the next real cadence-slice by harmonic+melodic fit, and a MOTIF memory returns earlier themes at recapitulation points, so minute 5 is *developed* from minute 1, not shuffled. Every sound is a real decoded slice through `createSafeMaster` (0 synth) — the machine only re-arranges his own playing. This is `15376-movement` / `15392-recur`, cycle 1184's DEEP ×2 (DOM/type living-score vs Canvas2D recurrence-map surfaces). Chain visible: this dive → §1184 build. Named refs in-README: Hawley 2026 (time-scale hierarchy), David Cope's EMI (recombinant composition), Lamere's Infinite Jukebox (real-slice re-sequencing).

## 2026-08-18 (cycle 1183) — Ephemeral audio / decay-through-listening: a recording that is spent, not played (+ phrase-boundary slicing so the cost is cadence-shaped)

**Dive.** Chasing the jury 2026-08-18's most-repeated, thinnest-lane provocation (#2: build something that is NOT a simulation — the conceptual/critical menu, named 0× in the window: *"a recording you can't hear without paying a cost"* literally spelled out in the verdict). Scanned the ephemeral-media / one-time-listening front + music phrase-segmentation for a design sharpener.

**Fresh signal (surprising).** A live 2026 current running through sound art: **ephemeral / decay-through-listening** — audio that degrades permanently *as you listen*, each work a single file with no copies, transience as the aesthetic rather than a bug. Surfaced via the *"Ephemeral & Fleeting — Modular Music of Japan"* compilation (Various Artists, released 2026-04-18, atoarchives — transience/fleeting-moment as the whole curatorial frame) and the broader ephemeral-audio idea that "you cannot hear it twice; the act of listening consumes it." Why it's surprising for THIS lab: 1,182 pieces have all been *additive* (press play, watch the recording bloom/grow/pulse — you are always *owed* the sound). None has ever made listening **cost** something, or made a recording **finite and consumable**. That inverts the lab's entire premise — and it's exactly the conceptual/critical register the jury says is 0×.

**Design sharpener (second source).** *Barwise Section Boundary Detection in Symbolic Music* (arXiv:2509.16566) + *Melodic Phrase Segmentation by DNNs* (arXiv:1811.05688) — the MIR line on **where a musical phrase actually ends**. Applied lightweight: slice the ledger of spendable phrases on **harmonic boundaries** (`loadTrackAnalysis().chords[]` onsets) and `summary.sections` seams rather than arbitrary even windows, so each unit of "cost" is a cadence-shaped phrase, not a clipped fragment — the resurrect-note "cadence-aware slice edges" cashed. (Even-window fallback stays, badged, when analysis is absent.)

**Could become a prototype that:** takes ONE real Karel take, cuts it into a finite **ledger of ~24–52 cadence-shaped phrases**, and starts **silent** — you are not owed the recording. To hear a phrase you **spend** it (press-and-hold past a commit threshold): it plays exactly once through `createSafeMaster`, then becomes a permanent **void** — struck out of the DOM grid, torn out of a Canvas2D timeline. No reset button. This is `15344-toll`, the ⭐⭐⭐⭐ conceptual/critical bank from §1182 — today's research chains straight into shipping it (case a→c hybrid: the bank came from the jury menu; this dive supplies the fresh ephemeral-media framing + the cadence-aware slicing that sharpens it). Chain visible: this dive → §1183 build of `15344-toll`.
