## §1151 — 2026-08-16 · Real-time low-latency music **source separation** is the 2026 turn (un-mix, don't just analyze)

**Sources (dates verified):** arXiv:2511.13146 *"Towards Practical Real-Time Low-Latency Music Source Separation"* (RT-STT, a single-path TFC-TDF U-Net with channel-expansion feature fusion for live demixing) + arXiv:2607.12872 *"Low-Latency Neural Models for Real-Time Music Enhancement"* (Jul 2026) + the **iCASSP 2026 Music Source Restoration Challenge** protocol (Multi-Mel-SNR / Zimtohrli / FAD-CLAP), all naming live remix / stream re-balancing / live performance as the target applications.

**Why it's surprising / on-point:** the recent lab window learned to *transform* one recording as a whole (conduct it, freeze it, walk inside the catalog). The 2026 frontier is the inverse verb — **pull one recording APART into its layers and let the listener re-balance them live**. This is the strongest possible answer to JURY #1 ("let Karel DO something, don't press Play and watch"): un-mixing is inherently a fader-in-hand, hands-on act. Full neural demixing needs a model (infeasible under rule "no heavy deps / no model in-browser"), BUT the classic **median-filtering Harmonic/Percussive Source Separation** (Fitzgerald, DAFx-10, 2010) is cheap, deterministic, and browser-native: STFT → horizontal median across time = harmonic mask, vertical median across frequency = percussive mask, soft Wiener masks → two ISTFT resyntheses. It gives the *interaction* the 2026 papers are chasing without a neural net, and it has never been shipped as a **rule-10 interactive instrument on his catalog** (only touched once, `6392-stemfield`, as a file-drop spatial mixer — never as median-filter HPSS you perform on his own piano).

**Could become a prototype that:** loads one of his real recordings, runs median-filter HPSS live, and hands Karel **faders to lift the melody off the pulse** — solo the harmonic layer to hear the sustained chords alone, solo the percussive layer to hear the attack/pedal transients, or dial any blend — with a WebGL2 field that shows the two layers being masked apart (harmonic ridges vs percussive sparks), cool ice/violet, off every jury ban. → built this cycle as `13904-unmixer` (case a, research→build chain).

## 2026-08-16 · §1150 — DEEP (ledger 1147 WIDE · 1148 DEEP · 1149 WIDE → **1150 DEEP** by alternation): **your POSITION is the mix — walk through / arrange a spatial world of your OWN recordings**

15-min dive (WebSearch, dates verified). The recent lab window has learned to let Karel *do* things to ONE recording at a time (conduct it, freeze it, morph between two) — but always as a single stream you manipulate. The live 2026 signal is the opposite move: **object-based immersive spatial audio**, where each sound is a discrete *object* placed in 3D and the listener's *position* is the mix. Fresh anchors: **"Immersive Audio in 2026: How Spatial Sound Tech is Elevating Live Events"** (ticketfairy, 2026 — concerts moving to object-based 360° mixing that *places each instrument in 3D space*) and the StartUs Insights 2026 spatial-audio scan naming **TALKARTIVE** (spatial guided *sound tours* — walk a site, the audio spatializes to where you stand) plus a wave of spatial-navigation / spatial-meditation startups. The through-line — *position = mix, each source is an independent spatialized object* — is exactly what Web Audio's HRTF `PannerNode` + `AudioListener` give for free in the browser, and the lab has **never loaded MANY of his recordings at once and spatialized each** (resonantrooms was a top-down floorplan with a moving listener, not his whole catalog as navigable objects). Secondary note: the recurrence/structure resurgence (**arXiv:2602.19816, Feb 2026, "Depth-Structured Music Recurrence"**) was considered but the self-similarity-matrix technique is already in the lab (`67-structure-viz`, `777-song-architecture`, `5384-cartograph`) and risks the jury-banned analysis-diagram template — rejected. Foundational `[older]`: Janet Cardiff & George Bures Miller (audio walks); Bernhard Leitner (sound architecture); acousmatic/BEAST spatial diffusion. **Could become a prototype that does X:** load his whole catalog at once, spatialize each recording as an HRTF object in a room/field/orbit, and let Karel **move through or arrange the space** — walking/drawing/orbiting continuously re-mixes his own music in 3D. Build implements the hook (case a) — DEEP races the concept 3 ways (first-person walk / drawn listening-path / radial orbit-sequencer).

## 2026-08-16 · §1149 — WIDE (ledger 1147 WIDE · 1148 DEEP → **1149 WIDE** by alternation): **the body, not the finger — move IN FRONT of your recording and conduct it**

15-min dive (WebSearch, dates verified). The recent lab window (canoncircle→dreambetween→callback→spectralsmear) let Karel *do* things with his recording — but every verb is a **finger on a screen** (pointer scrub, key press, drag-freeze). The 2026 gesture-music turn is embodied and camera-native: **SIGGRAPH 2026 Art Gallery (Los Angeles, Jul 2026, ~1 month old)** features *Gesture Flux* — mixed-reality gesture recognition fused with live performance (Budaixi glove-puppetry + dance) — and the technical-papers program foregrounds real-time interactive capture. The load-bearing *method* anchor is **"Real-Time Control of a Virtual Orchestra by Recognition of Conducting Gestures" (arXiv:2604.27957, Apr 2026)**: a National-Museum-of-Science-and-Technology (Sweden) dome installation where visitors conduct a *pre-recorded* orchestra with **NO baton/gloves — pure webcam optical flow**, deriving tempo + expression from the *overall quantity of user motion*. That paper was cited by §1144 `conductorwell`, but conductorwell faked it with a **pointer** — the lab has **never shipped live camera OR optical flow**. Supporting fresh signal: *Gesture2Music / "Rhythm in the Air"* (arXiv:2511.00793) — MediaPipe body/hand landmarks → causal-TCN → real-time control (confirms the low-latency in-browser feasibility). Foundational `[older]`: Horn–Schunck / Lucas–Kanade optical flow; Macklin & Müller *Position Based Fluids* (2013); Runions et al. *Modeling Trees with a Space Colonization Algorithm* (2007). **Could become a prototype that does X:** move your hand/body in front of the webcam and its *optical flow* conducts the tempo + intensity of your own recording — the literal webcam method the Swedish dome uses, which the lab only ever approximated with a mouse. Build implements the hook (case a) — WIDE races it against two other embodied/physical inputs (tilt-poured liquid, grown botanical) so the fire also breaks the finger-on-glass monoculture on three fronts, not one.

## 2026-08-15 · §1148 — DEEP (ledger 1146 DEEP · 1147 WIDE → **1148 DEEP** by alternation): **sound as a PLASTIC MATERIAL — grab / freeze / stretch / scratch his recording with your hands**

The through-line of the 2026 real-time-audio turn is no longer "the model responds" (that was §1147's co-performance framing) but the performer **physically deforming recorded sound as a material** — grabbing an instant and stretching it, freezing a chord into an infinite shimmer, scratching a phrase back and forth. Three converging, dated signals:

- **Latent Granular Resynthesis using Neural Audio Codecs — arXiv:2507.19202 (Jul 2025).** Reworks granular synthesis at the *latent-vector* level: encode a source corpus into latent segments = a "granular codebook" you resynthesize from. The idea we borrow (without the neural codec — rule 10 keeps the audio literally his): treat ONE of Karel's recordings as a corpus of grains that a performer plays *as material*, not a timeline you press Play on. https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.19202
- **The 2026 real-time tactile-granular tooling turn** — the granular-plugin field's 2026 roundups (kvraudio "Best Granular Synthesis Plugins in 2026"; GRFX, a real-time granular processor built around *tactile* single-knob grain-density / spread / smear control for live performance). The centre of gravity moved from offline sound-design to live, hands-on grain manipulation. https://www.kvraudio.com/the-best-granular-synthesis-plugins-in-2026-from-theory-to-practice
- **NIME 2026 live-gesture-control program** — e.g. "Shifting Time Scales: Supporting Live Gesture-Controlled Generative Music with Speculative Execution" and wearable gesture→sound mappings (Layika). The live-performance research community is squarely on *gesture directly manipulates sound in real time* — the verb the concept jury has demanded for four straight cycles (interaction over analysis). https://nime.org/papers/
- Foundational anchors for the DSP itself (tagged `[older, foundational]`): Curtis Roads *Microsound* (granular synthesis); Flanagan & Golden 1966 / Dolson 1986 (phase vocoder, spectral freeze). Client-side Web-Audio time-stretch is genuinely hard — Dias & Matos, *Time-Stretching & Pitch-Shifting with the Web Audio API: Where are we at?* confirms phase-vocoder + overlap-add are the only viable browser paths and JS implementations remain scarce/experimental — so building a hands-on granular-OLA scrubber AND a phase-vocoder freeze in-browser is itself a non-trivial contribution.

**Honesty note:** the strongest *specific papers* here are 2024–2025, not the last 30 days; the genuinely-2026 signal is the tooling roundups + the NIME 2026 program. The area (real-time granular/spectral performance in-browser) is fast-moving and under-shipped in the lab, so this is a live thread, not recycled training-data.

**Could become a prototype (this cycle's build, case a → DEEP):** *"Play your own recording as a plastic material."* Pick one of his real tracks; grab it and **scrub/scratch** the playhead like a turntablist, **hold to freeze** an instant into a sustained grain/spectral shimmer, **drag to time-stretch** a chord into a slow bloom — always in his own piano tone (rule 10, zero synth). Raced three ways: `13728-tapewarp` (time-domain granular-OLA scrub on a three.js 3D waveform ribbon) · `13744-grainloom` (two-thumb SVG-DOM filmstrip, density×pitch-scatter grain cloud) · `13760-spectralsmear` (frequency-domain phase-vocoder freeze/smear on a WebGL2 spectrogram of his STFT).

## 2026-08-15 · §1147 — WIDE (ledger 1145 WIDE · 1146 DEEP → **1147 WIDE** by alternation): **human–AI *co-performance* as the missing verb — three ways to PLAY his own recordings, not watch them**

15-min dive (WebSearch, dates verified). Freshest, most on-point: [**"Towards Real-Time Human–AI Musical Co-Performance: Accompaniment Generation with Latent Diffusion Models and MAX/MSP" (arXiv:2604.07612, 2026)**](https://arxiv.org/html/2604.07612) — a live-performance framework where a generative model *responds to a stream of the performer's context audio in real time* (MAX/MSP front-end ↔ Python inference over OSC/UDP). Sits in a live 2026 interaction-first cluster: [SnakeSynth (continuous 2D input → variable-length generative sound with strum/bow/pluck gesture analogies)](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2307.05830) and [a real-time human-in-the-loop gesture→music control framework (arXiv:2504.19460)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.19460). **Why surprising / the hook:** the through-line of every 2026 system above is *co-performance* — the human acts, the sound responds — which is exactly the verb JURY 2026-08-15 #1 has demanded for four straight cycles ("let Karel **do** something — play, manipulate, perform, re-voice — not sit and watch"). The lab's answer: make his OWN 16-track catalog the material a performer bends, with NO generated audio at all (rule 10) — three divergent instruments, each a different *way to play his recordings*. **Chains → today's WIDE build (case a):** three interaction-first briefs, all real-catalog via `loadRealTrackBuffer` through `createSafeMaster`, none passive/analysis-diagram/Canvas2D-primary/warm-ember/cosmic/frag-shader (all jury-banned this cycle) —
- **`13696-callback`** — **[WON]** call-and-response **duet**: his phrase plays, then it's your turn, and the keys you answer on are voiced by pitch-shifted **grains of his own recording** (concatenative / corpus instrument; SVG timeline). Refs: IRCAM **CataRT** / D. Schwarz corpus-based concatenative synthesis; [**The Concatenator** (arXiv:2411.04366)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.04366) Bayesian real-time musaicing.
- **`13680-tapewarp`** — [banked ⭐⭐⭐] perform his recording like tape: grab a 3D waveform ribbon (three.js) and scrub / freeze / reverse / stutter via real-time **granular time-stretch** (overlap-add, pitch-preserving). Solo turntablist of his piano.
- **`13712-canontabs`** — [banked ⭐⭐] resurrect the multi-user shared-clock canon (`11888-chorusrooms`) on his real catalog (JURY #4): **BroadcastChannel** leader-elected clock turns each open tab into a voice; single-tab layered fallback.

**Honest freshness note:** the co-performance paper's *framing* is the current-2026 turn (genuinely on-point); the enabling machinery — concatenative grain playback, granular OLA time-stretch, BroadcastChannel leader election / Reich-phase canon — is foundational (flagged). First-in-lab: three *performance* instruments built entirely from his own recorded audio in one fire, and specifically a **playable pitched instrument voiced by his own grains inside a duet frame** (the winner), a verb the recent window lacked.

## 2026-08-15 · §1146 — DEEP (ledger 1143 WIDE · 1144 DEEP · 1145 WIDE → **1146 DEEP** by alternation): **sound as an action-conditioned world model → make the reservoir-dream STEERABLE, on his real audio**

- **Source (fresh, <30 days):** [Music-JEPA: Learning a World Model of Sound from Action (arXiv:2607.22000, Ziyu Wang, Jul 24 2026)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22000) — 22 days old at review. Frames **piano sound as an action-conditioned system**: a joint-embedding predictive world model learns to predict how sound evolves *from an action*, rather than modeling raw audio. Piano-specific, which is unusually on-brand for Karel's catalog.
- **Why it's surprising / the hook:** the lab's reservoir line (echofold → recallorbit → dreammedley) built genuine tiny "minds" that hold and dream a phrase — but every one is **passive**: it auto-navigates and you *watch*. Music-JEPA's action-conditioned framing is the missing verb: treat the visitor's steering as the *action* and the sound as the world model's *response*. That reframes the 5/5 `12976-dreammedley` from a thing you press-Play-and-watch into a thing you **perform** — the exact pivot JURY #1 has demanded for four cycles ("interaction over analysis"), and it lands on the jury's #3 named target (give dreammedley a rule-10 cycle on his *real* recorded audio, not the FM-resynthesized pitch contours it uses today).
- **Could become a prototype that does X (this cycle, case a → shipped):** *"Dream between your OWN recordings, and steer it."* A reservoir/latent navigator whose anchors are his real recordings, where **every sound is his real piano** (concatenative / spectral-morph / multi-source-granular of his actual audio — no FM/synth) and **Karel drags a cursor to steer** the dream (hands-off → it auto-wanders). Raced 3 real-audio morph engines; shipped `13664-dreambetween` (reservoir navigator → multi-source granular attention cloud). Supporting technique refs (foundational, for the morph engines): CataRT / D. Schwarz corpus-based concatenative synthesis; [The Concatenator (arXiv:2411.04366)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.04366) Bayesian real-time musaicing; [SoundMorpher (arXiv:2410.02144)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.02144) perceptually-uniform morphing (SPDP); [Echo State Transformer (arXiv:2507.02917)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.02917) reservoirs-as-attention-slots.

## 2026-08-15 · §1145 — WIDE (ledger 1142 DEEP · 1143 WIDE · 1144 DEEP → **1145 WIDE** by alternation): **collaborative canon on his real catalog — the harmonic-consensus room**

15-min dive (WebSearch/WebFetch, dates verified). Freshest, most on-point (<30 days): [**D. Shin, "Real-Time Collaborative Generative Music Jamming on a Video Sharing Platform," Technical Disclosure Commons, Aug 5, 2026**](https://www.tdcommons.org/dpubs_series/11270/) — a multi-user room where many participants jointly co-create ONE evolving track from heterogeneous simultaneous inputs (text, hummed melody, MIDI), reconciled by a **"harmonic consensus stage"** that "projects these inputs into a shared harmonic latent space, resolving conflicts among competing inputs into a single harmonic guide that fixes a unified key, tempo, and chord progression." Sits with a live 2026 collaborative-web-audio cluster — [IEEE 2026 "Real-Time Collaborative Music Creation on the Web: exploiting Web Audio Modules"](https://ieeexplore.ieee.org/document/11325761/) and the CRDT-synced `sequencer.party` lineage (state sync via conflict-free replicated data types + a designated host as timing authority). **Why surprising for us:** the JURY 2026-08-15 verdict (#4) explicitly asked to *resurrect the multi-user shared-clock canon (`11888-chorusrooms`) with Karel's real catalog instead of oscillators* — the freshest infrastructure in the window spent on the wrong audio source (a rule-10 miss). The Shin disclosure upgrades exactly that: not just a shared clock, but a **consensus stage that keeps layered voices consonant** — the missing musical glue that turns "several tabs playing at once" into an actual canon. Multi-user is a thin category (0× in the last 10) and the lab has **never** shipped a collaborative piece built on his real recordings. **Chains → today's WIDE build (case a):** three divergent interaction-first briefs, all real-catalog via `loadRealTrackBuffer` through `createSafeMaster`, none passive/analysis/Canvas2D/warm/cosmic/frag-shader — **`13568-canoncircle`** (multi-tab `BroadcastChannel` leader-elected shared clock + harmonic-consensus phrase alignment of his real recorded phrases → a live round; inline-SVG canon clock; cashes jury #4 + the Shin disclosure) [WON], **`13584-scatterbox`** (a physics THROW instrument — self-built 2D rigid-body sim whose every bounce triggers a grain of his piano; three.js) [banked ⭐⭐⭐], **`13600-beatcarve`** (grab any region of his recording and perform a beat from it — lookahead-scheduled beat-repeat/stutter/reverse engine; WebGL2 waveform) [banked ⭐⭐]. **Honest freshness note:** the Shin disclosure is 10 days old (genuinely current); BroadcastChannel leader-election + phase/canon (Reich) are foundational — but a *harmonic-consensus collaborative canon over his own catalog* is first-in-lab and directly cashes the same-day jury #4.

## 2026-08-15 · §1144 — DEEP (ledger 1141 WIDE · 1142 DEEP · 1143 WIDE → **1144 DEEP** by alternation): **conduct your own recording — the recording is the orchestra, the hand is the baton**

15-min dive (WebSearch, dates verified). Freshest, most on-point (<6 months, and the *framing* is a 2026 turn): [**"Real-Time Control of a Virtual Orchestra by Recognition of Conducting Gestures" (arXiv:2604.27957, April 2026)**](https://arxiv.org/html/2604.27957) — a **180° dome museum installation** that lets visitors *conduct* a pre-recorded professional-orchestra performance, the gesture driving speed / volume / balance in real time. Sits with a live 2026 cluster: [Gesture2Music (arXiv:2511.00793)](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2511.00793) low-latency continuous gesture→music, and the expressive-timing/rubato-rendering strand (RenderBox arXiv:2502.07711; RenCon 2025 arXiv:2605.02059; *Rubato* transcription arXiv:2605.24291). **Why surprising for us:** the JURY 2026-08-15 verdict just flagged the lab's new rut — four consecutive "read a dimension of his playing → draw a diagram → press Play → *watch*" pieces (vocabularygraph/rubatoline/handreader/resonancefield) — and asked (#1) to "let Karel **do** something: play, manipulate, perform, **conduct**, re-voice." The dome paper is the literal answer: conducting a *pre-recorded* performance is exactly rule-10-shaped — his 16-track catalog IS the "pre-recorded orchestra," and his gesture becomes the baton. The lab has ~13k prototypes and has **never** given him a way to *perform* his own recording (striketemple §1143 let him play *alongside* it; this lets him bend *it*). The enabling technique is a **real-time pitch-preserving time-stretch** so the hand can rush/hold/reverse the tempo while pitch stays nailed to his piano — cleanest in Web Audio as **synchronous granular / overlap-add** (reading `AudioBuffer` slices at `playbackRate=1` preserves pitch; advance the read-head at the conducted rate → stretch time), lineage [PhaVoRIT / Karrer, Lee & Borchers 2006](https://hci.rwth-aachen.de/publications/karrer2006a.pdf). **Honest freshness note:** the dome paper is April 2026 (genuinely current); the granular/phase-vocoder time-stretch machinery is foundational (flagged) — but a *conductor instrument over his own catalog with a real conductor's beat* is first-in-lab, and it directly cashes the same-day jury #1. **Chains → today's DEEP build (case a):** ONE concept — *conduct your own recording (tempo/rubato + dynamics), pitch unchanged* — raced 3 different-in-kind interaction models, all real-catalog via `loadRealTrackBuffer` through `createSafeMaster` + `loadTrackAnalysis` note-roll, all sharing the granular-OLA engine: **`13536-conductorwell`** (three.js perspective well; a **circular beat-conducting** gesture — orbit angular-velocity = tempo, radius = dynamics; motes stream into depth), **`13520-conductorline`** (WebGL2 note-river; pointer-velocity SCRUB baton, single `uReadPos` uniform flows+reverses on the GPU), **`13552-conductorbreath`** (inline-SVG; a single vertical BREATH axis, and the note spacing = the visible tempo). Curated to one winner (**conductorwell** — the boldest realization of the actual verb "conduct"), the two others banked to IDEAS §1144 as its declared cycle-2/cycle-3 deepenings. **Next hooks:** true 4/4 beat-pattern recognition snapping to his downbeats; a phase-vocoder engine option for cleaner extreme stretch; recordable conducted takes; and the biggest standing lever — a real-device/sound-on pass (does the beat-gesture *feel* like beating time on his hardware? does three.js render?).

## 2026-08-15 — Cycle 1143 research dive (WIDE)

**Primary finding (freshest, <30 days): "Sonify Anything: Towards Context-Aware Sonic Interactions in AR"** — arXiv:2508.01789 (Aug 2026, cs.SD/HCI). https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.01789 — Argues interactive sound should be *context-aware*: a struck/handled object should get a physically-modeled **modal** voice that fits its surroundings, not a canned sample. Surprising bit for us: it reframes "what does this object sound like?" as a live, context-conditioned modal-synthesis question. **Could become a prototype that:** lets you strike on-screen bells/bowls whose modal tuning is continuously conditioned by the *chord Karel is playing right now* in a real recording — a duet where your percussion is always in his key. → became **13488-striketemple** this cycle.

**Secondary (May 2026, art-technique anchor): "ChladniSonify: A Visual-Acoustic Mapping Method for Chladni Patterns in New Media Art Creation"** — arXiv:2605.09846. https://arxiv.org/pdf/2605.09846 — Formalizes the strict correspondence between a plate's vibration eigenmodes and excitation frequency as a mapping method for installation art. **Could become a prototype that:** drives a Chladni nodal-line particle sim from the live spectrum of Karel's recording, so his harmony sculpts sand-figure geometry. → became **13472-cymatics** this cycle.

**Landscape scan (dated):** arXiv:2606.10627 *Profy* (Jun 2026, interpretable visualization of expertise-dependent piano motor skills) and arXiv:2606.12282 *PianoKontext* (Jun 2026, flow-matching expressive-performance rendering) — both strong but both *analytical/practice-facing*, which is exactly the "portrait of his playing" cluster the lab is over-invested in (resonancefield/vocabularygraph/rubatoline/handreader). Deliberately steered AWAY from them this cycle toward experiential/material/instrument directions. Also logged: arXiv:2603.10240 *nlm: Real-Time Non-linear Modal Synthesis in Max* (Mar 2026) and arXiv:2210.15306 *Rigid-Body Sound Synthesis with Differentiable Modal Resonators* [older, foundational] — modal-synthesis lineage backing striketemple. Cross-synthesis lineage for crossweave is foundational (Dudley channel vocoder 1939; Wishart *Audible Design*; Flanagan–Golden phase vocoder 1966) [older, foundational].

**Chain (today's research → today's build):** Sonify Anything (Aug-2026) → 13488-striketemple; ChladniSonify (May-2026) → 13472-cymatics. Third brief 13504-crossweave is grounded in foundational cross-synthesis, not a 30-day source (honest note).

## 2026-08-15 · §1142 — DEEP (ledger 1139 WIDE · 1140 DEEP · 1141 WIDE → **1142 DEEP** by alternation): **sympathetic resonance — the WHOLE instrument ringing, not just the struck string** — 2026 piano research is reconstructing continuous *touch/key-motion* beyond discrete MIDI, and the lab (named "Resonance") had never drawn the sympathetic afterglow that is literally its namesake

15-min dive (WebSearch, dates verified). The live thread: **2026 piano-performance research is reaching past discrete note events toward the CONTINUOUS physical behavior of the instrument — touch, key-motion, the sound that keeps living after the key.** The keystone this cycle is [**"Visualising Pianists' Touch: Transcribing Expressive Piano Performance from Audio to Piano Key Motion" (CHI 2026, doi:10.1145/3772318.3791621)**](https://doi.org/10.1145/3772318.3791621): it transcribes continuous **key-depression trajectories** directly from audio — physically-grounded performance data that "better reflects how performers shape sound than typical MIDI," letting both experts and non-experts *perceive* expressive differences that discrete onsets throw away. It sits with the embodied-performance strand from §1141 (SKY-Piano arXiv:2607.27296, Profy arXiv:2606.10627) and high-resolution 1 kHz key-motion sensing that captures "movement, timing and release, rather than only the acoustic outcome." **Why surprising for us:** the lab has ~13k prototypes and, over the last four cycles, drew Karel's **harmony** (13328-vocabularygraph), **timing** (13248-rubatoline), catalog as **space** (13216-resonantrooms) and his **hands as motion** (13360-handreader) — but has **never drawn the thing the product is literally NAMED after: sympathetic resonance.** When he strikes a key, every undamped string sharing a partial rings in sympathy — the "afterglow" that ordinary note-visualizers (one dot per struck note) completely miss. Modeling the whole 88-string instrument as a bank of *coupled* damped resonators, excited by his REAL note-roll, makes visible which strings his playing wakes without him touching them. That's the deepest cash of absolute rule 10 (his actual `loadTrackAnalysis.notes[]` MIDI pitch/time/velocity → a physical coupling model, not an FFT of noise) and the cleanest thematic fit the lab has. **Honest freshness note:** the CHI 2026 touch paper is genuinely current (2026); the acoustics lineage (sympathetic string resonance, Helmholtz coupled resonators, partial-overlap coupling) is foundational (flagged) — but the *framing* (continuous touch/resonance as the modality, past discrete MIDI) is a 2026 turn, and a coupled-resonator "afterglow" reading of his catalog is first-in-lab. The coupling is a plausibility model of sympathetic resonance, NOT a physical measurement of his instrument — flagged honestly, same browser-feasible register recent dives use. **Chains → today's DEEP build (case a):** ONE concept — *Karel's real recording lighting up the WHOLE piano as 88 coupled resonators, so you see the sympathetic afterglow* — raced 3 different-in-kind renderers, all real-catalog via `loadRealTrackBuffer` through `createSafeMaster` + `loadTrackAnalysis` note-roll excitation: **`13424-sympathetics`** (raw WebGL2 instanced 88-string bank, sympathetic bleed lighting harmonically-related strings, CPU coupling), **`13440-resonancefield`** (WebGPU-compute resonator lattice + coupling matrix on GPU, rendered as a blooming energy field, CPU fallback), **`13456-resonancehall`** (three.js 3D hall where sympathetic energy travels along glowing edges to partner strings in DEPTH). Curated to one winner. **Next hooks (deepening):** infer pedaling from note-roll legato/overlap so the sympathetic tail lengthens when he holds the pedal; a real-device pass asking whether the model's afterglow matches what he hears his piano actually do; overlay the CHI 2026 continuous key-motion (audio→key-depth) so struck strings show real touch depth, not a binary onset.

## 2026-08-15 · §1141 — WIDE (ledger 1138 DEEP · 1139 WIDE · 1140 DEEP → **1141 WIDE** by alternation): **piano performance as embodied MOTION, not just sound** — 2026 MIR has turned the pianist's *body/physicality* into a first-class, captured-and-visualized modality, and the lab has drawn Karel's harmony, timing, and space but has never drawn his PLAYING as motion

15-min dive (WebSearch, dates verified). The live thread: **2026 piano-MIR is capturing and visualizing the *physical performance* — hands, motion, muscles, expertise — as its own modality alongside audio/MIDI.** The keystone is [**SKY-Piano: A Multimodal Piano Performance Dataset (arXiv:2607.27296, ISMIR 2026)**](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27296): 11 hours of synchronized **motion capture + multi-view video + audio + MIDI + MusicXML** from 7 professional and 12 amateur pianists — the field is now treating *how the hands move* as data worth modeling, not a byproduct. It sits with [**Profy: Interpretable Visualization of Expertise-Dependent Motor Skills (arXiv:2606.10627, 2026)**](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10627) (visualizing the physicality that separates expert from novice piano technique) and [**Pianist Transformer: Expressive Piano Performance Rendering (arXiv:2512.02652)**](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.02652) (rendering the *expressive*, physical layer of a performance). **Why surprising for us:** the lab has ~13k prototypes and, over the last four cycles alone, has drawn Karel's **harmony** (13328-vocabularygraph, the PageRank chord graph), his **timing/rubato** (13248-rubatoline), and his catalog as **space** (13216-resonantrooms) — but has **never drawn his PLAYING as embodied MOTION**: the choreography of his two hands moving across the keys, reconstructed from his real note-roll. That's the next expressive dimension the 2026 literature is opening, and it's a clean cash of absolute rule 10 (`loadTrackAnalysis.notes[]` — his ACTUAL MIDI pitch/time/duration/velocity — reconstructed into hand kinematics, not an FFT of noise). **Honest freshness note:** SKY-Piano/Profy (2606/2607 = mid-2026) are ~1–2 months old (genuinely current); a full mocap solver is not browser-feasible, so the build reconstructs a *plausible* two-hand choreography from the note-roll (a pitch→key-position + register-split heuristic), flagged honestly as an approximation, not a mocap replay. **Chains → today's WIDE build (case a):** brief **`13360-handreader`** realizes it — reconstruct Karel's two-hand keyboard choreography in three.js from his real note-roll, keys depressing on his real onsets, synced to catalog playback (off the banned Canvas2D/frag-shader/cosmic/mic tags — three.js embodied stage). Raced WIDE against two unrelated directions/renderers to attack the "too similar" note: **`13376-scoreterminal`** (his music read out as an Ikeda-clinical DOM/CSS data-terminal — chord/key/register/dynamics as animated monospace type; Ryoji Ikeda *data.matics*) and **`13392-voiceweave`** (his counterpoint separated into braided SVG voice-strands by pitch-proximity streaming; David Huron *Voice Leading*). **Next hooks (deepening):** overlay real velocity as strike-force so you *see* his dynamics in the hands; a two-take comparison of the same piece's choreography; fuse handreader's hands with voiceweave's voice-coloring so each finger carries its voice's color.

## 2026-08-15 · §1140 — DEEP (ledger 1137 W · 1138 DEEP · 1139 WIDE → **1140 DEEP** by alternation): **the harmonic GRAPH as the subject** — 2026 MIR has a toolkit that builds a composer's "harmonic graph" by running PageRank centrality on their chord-transition network, and the lab had never drawn the *structure* of Karel's harmony, only its texture

15-min dive (WebSearch + WebFetch, dates verified). The live thread: **symbolic-music analysis is moving from sequence to NETWORK** — treating a body of work as a directed graph of chord→chord transitions and reading its *topology* to characterize a composer. The keystone is [**vega-mir, "An information-theoretic toolkit for symbolic music, with applications to harmonic graphs and rubato spectra" (arXiv:2605.16539, submitted 15 May 2026)**](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16539): it builds **harmonic graphs** by computing network-centrality measures — specifically **PageRank on chord-transition networks** — so the chords a composer structurally *leans on* surface as the highest-centrality nodes, and it extends the same construction to n-gram, motif, and rhythmic-pattern graphs (and, tying to last cycle's ship, "rubato spectra" = spectral analysis of timing-deviation curves). It sits in a genuine lineage: network-theoretic symbolic-music analysis ([*Analysis and Visualization of Musical Structure using Networks*, arXiv:2404.15208](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.15208)), the **Tonnetz / Euler tonal network** (Euler 1739, foundational), and force-directed layout ([Fruchterman & Reingold 1991, foundational]). **Why surprising for us:** the dream lab has ~13k prototypes and reads Karel's chords constantly — as color, as reverb, as onset triggers — but has **never drawn the SHAPE of his harmonic vocabulary itself**: which chords are *central* to how he composes, which transitions are the spine, how the network re-centers as you move through his whole catalog. PageRank-centrality is the difference between showing the *sequence* of his harmony (a texture) and showing its *structure* (an insight) — the deepest cash of absolute rule 10 ("visuals that genuinely READ his music — harmony, form — not an FFT of noise"), because the math *is* the reading. **Honest freshness note:** vega-mir (2605 = May 2026) is ~3 months old and the PageRank / Tonnetz / Fruchterman–Reingold primitives are foundational (flagged) — but the *framing* (a composer's harmonic graph read by centrality, live) is a 2026 turn and the piece-idea is first-in-lab. **Chains → today's DEEP build (case a):** ONE concept — *Karel's whole 16-track catalog as one long-form organism with MEMORY* — raced 3 technical approaches, curated **`13328-vocabularygraph`** as the winner: it accretes his real chord-transition graph across all 16 tracks (never reset), runs live damped PageRank power-iterations to size nodes, and self-organizes them with a force layout — implementing vega-mir's harmonic-graph construction live from his recordings. Raced against `13296-catalogsediment` (WebGPU-compute never-reset Gray-Scott sediment fed by his harmony — banked ⭐⭐⭐) and `13312-corebloom` (a scrollable stratigraphic "core sample" of the catalog — banked ⭐⭐); winner chosen because the centrality math IS the insight where the other two map harmony onto a texture. **Next hooks (deepening, folded into the winner's README):** render the graph over a slow RD ground driven by the PageRank distribution (fuse the sediment approach); a stratigraphic time-scrubber that replays how the graph grew (fuse the core-sample approach); history-aware layout so a returning chord lands where it lived 40 min ago; extend to n-gram / motif graphs per vega-mir; a real-device pass asking whether the centrality ranking matches how Karel hears his own harmony.

## 2026-08-15 · §1139 — WIDE (ledger 1135 W · 1136 D · 1137 W · 1138 DEEP → **1139 WIDE** by alternation): **making expressive rubato itself the subject** — 2026 MIR has started building tools to SEE the push-and-pull of a pianist's timing, and Karel's Covid-era solo piano is rubato to the core, yet the lab has onset-detection *primitives* but has never built a piece *about* expressive timing

15-min dive (WebSearch, dates checked). The live thread: **rubato — expressive tempo deviation — is a 2026 MIR frontier being made *visible*.** [*Rubato: Transcribing Piano Music with Timestamps* (arXiv:2605.24291, 2026)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24291) introduces an encoder-decoder ("Rubato") + a textual representation ("InterMo") that produces **timestamped** piano sheet music from audio, explicitly so listeners can *visualize rubato*, learners can diagnose timing choices against the written note, and musicologists can compare performance styles. A companion 2026 strand ([*performance-expressiveness modeling*, Sci Rep s41598-026-44473-w](https://www.nature.com/articles/s41598-026-44473-w)) distinguishes **intentional rubato from uncontrolled timing variation** via rhythmic-stability metrics — experts show *lower* stability during expressive passages yet keep phrase-level coherence, unlike novice instability. The shipping real-time engine for this is causal: a **Predominant Local Pulse** tempo curve run online ([Grosche & Müller PLP; TISMIR 2024 "zero-latency real-time beat tracking," 10.5334/tismir.189](https://transactions.ismir.net/articles/10.5334/tismir.189) adapts the PLP kernel to causal-only frames with beat lookahead) over a **spectral-flux novelty function** onset detector ([Simon Dixon, "Onset Detection Revisited"](https://www.ofai.at/papers/oefai-tr-2006-12.pdf), foundational). **Why surprising for us:** the dream lab has ~13k prototypes and does own onset primitives (`4264-lucent/analysis.ts`, `402-kids-steady-walk/onset.ts`), but every one uses onsets to *trigger a visual* — **none has ever made the expressive timing the SUBJECT**: drawing the elastic tempo curve of a real recording and separating "he's pushing/pulling on purpose" from "steady time." Karel's *Welcome Home* is rubato-rich solo piano written through Covid — this reads his ACTUAL playing, not an FFT of noise, the cleanest cash of absolute rule 10. **Honest freshness note:** the arXiv Rubato paper (2605 = May 2026) is ~3 months old and the causal-PLP / Dixon onset lineage is foundational (flagged `[foundational]`), not last-7-days — but the *framing* (rubato-as-subject, intentional-vs-unintentional stability) is a genuinely 2026 turn in MIR and the specific piece-idea is first-in-lab. **Chains → today's WIDE build (case a):** brief **`13248-rubatoline`** realizes it — real-time causal spectral-flux onset + a lightweight online PLP tempo curve + an IOI-variance "rhythmic stability" readout, drawn as an **ink-on-paper SVG notation** line that bunches and spreads with his rubato (off the banned Canvas2D/frag-shader/cosmic/mic tags). Raced WIDE against two unrelated directions (a tilt-conducted three.js weave over a real track; a long-form WebGPU reaction-diffusion garden seeded by his real chords) to attack the "too similar" note with three different renderers in one fire. **Next hooks (if resurrected/deepened):** overlay the piece's *notated* onsets from `loadTrackAnalysis.notes[]` against the detected onsets to show human-vs-grid deviation directly; a two-recording rubato comparison (same piece, two takes); export the tempo curve as a shareable "timing fingerprint."

## 2026-08-15 · §1138 — DEEP (ledger 1135 W · 1136 D · 1137 W → **1138 DEEP**): **head-tracked binaural sound-field navigation** — the listener's own position moving through a field of sources — is a live 2026 frontier, and the lab had never made his catalog a *space you walk through*; so this DEEP shipped it

Why surprising for us: the dream lab has ~13k prototypes and a *spatial-choir* family (`308-orbit-choir`, `677-presence-field`, ~32 pieces) where sound orbits YOU, but **his own recorded catalog had never been laid out AS a navigable space you move a listener through** — and jury-2026-08-14 #4 explicitly ordered "spend a DEEP cycle on spatial/embodied — the mandate's top thin category, untouched." It is also the cleanest cash of the standing **absolute rule 10** (real catalog audio — recent MIDI/synth ships had drifted off it) and jury #1 (audio from OUTSIDE the piece — his real recordings, not a self-drone). **Honest freshness note:** HRTF `PannerNode` + `AudioListener` are foundational Web Audio; the *fresh* layer is the 2026 head-tracked-binaural-under-rotation research + the current immersive-soundscape practice — flagged honestly, same browser-feasible register recent dives use. No false first-in-lab billed (HRTF panning + convolution reverb both pre-exist widely — 81 files use `createVoidReverb`); the fresh value is the **combination**: his complete catalog spatialized as an architecture you navigate. **Chains → today's DEEP (case a):** ONE concept — *Karel's whole catalog as a walkable, head-panned listening SPACE* — raced 3 spatial approaches, each real-catalog + HRTF + Canvas2D + non-cosmic: **`13216-resonantrooms`** (five collections = five convolution-reverb ROOMS in one building, doorway equal-power bleed between acoustically-distinct spaces — Cardiff *Forty-Part Motet* / Lucier — SHIPPED), **`13232-atlas`** (catalog laid out by musical KEY on the circle of fifths, drag-pan the map = walk the terrain, `loadTrackAnalysis`-driven — banked ⭐⭐⭐ RESURRECT-FIRST), **`13200-listeningroom`** (open gallery floor-plan HRTF soundwalk — Schafer — banked ⭐⭐⭐). Shipped resonantrooms as the biggest concept + strongest reference + most subsystems (5) + most installation/Tauri-grade; banked the other two to IDEAS §1138. **NOT ear/device-verified** (headless — no speakers): whether the five reverbs read as genuinely distinct spaces and the doorway bleed feels like standing between two rooms wants Karel's headphones; the seeded muted auto-tour (room→doorway→room, alive on the first painted frame) is the review stand-in. **Next hooks:** verify the atlas's analysis coverage on a real device then resurrect it; fuse listeningroom's per-zone soundwalk INTO resonantrooms so the whole catalog is one continuous building; a true measured-IR convolution (a real room's impulse response) instead of the synthetic void tail.

## 2026-08-15 · §1137 — WIDE (ledger 1133 W · 1134 D · 1135 W · 1136 D → **1137 WIDE**): the 2026 frontier names **interaction modality as a first-class design axis** for live music systems — and the lab's monoculture is a monoculture of ONE modality (analyser-of-a-self-drone), so this WIDE fire deliberately spreads across three under-used input modalities

15-min dive (WebSearch + WebFetch, dates checked). The most useful current thread: **[A Design Space for Live Music Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.05064)** (arXiv:2602.05064, **CHI '26, submitted Feb 6 2026**) organizes real-time interactive music systems along four axes — **musical role** (harmonic/rhythmic/melodic), **interaction modality** (audio-listening, gestural, mixed input), **temporal awareness** (beat-tracking, phrase detection, score-following) and **adaptation** (learning the performer's style). The surprising-for-us reading: the jury-2026-08-14 "cosmic self-playing-shader monoculture" is, in this taxonomy's terms, a collapse onto a SINGLE cell of the grid — audio-analyser interaction modality reading a self-generated drone — so the paper's own prescription ("identify unexplored combinations of agent capabilities") is a direct instruction to vary the *interaction modality*. Corroborating the external-data half: real-time **data sonification** is a live 2026 practice, not a museum piece — **[Helioradar AV](https://av.helioradar.com/)** streams live NOAA space-weather over a WebSocket into a generative ambient soundscape at sub-minute latency, and **[Sonic Weather](https://www.international-sound-directory.com/project/sonic-weather/)** / the [GSA 2025 weather-sonification showcase](https://gsashowcase.net/work/real-time-weather-data-sonification/) turn live meteorological variables into a navigable auditory experience. **Honest freshness note:** the design-space paper's *anticipation* mechanic was already cashed by the lab (`10568-thrumline`, a Web-MIDI jazz-comping agent), so this cycle uses the paper as a *lens* on its interaction-modality axis, not a novel re-implementation — flagged honestly. **Chains → today's WIDE (case a/c).** One research lens (vary the interaction modality; sonify something outside the piece) seeded 3 divergent explorers, each on a DIFFERENT under-used input modality off every jury-banned tag and off the over-used mic-primary of the last-10: **`13152-tiltbowl`** (device-orientation → tilt-steered coupled-modal singing-bowl, energy poured around the rim; Rossing's bell/bowl mode-pair beating), **`13168-preparedchance`** (Web-MIDI/QWERTY → Karplus-Strong prepared-string voices re-composed by a seeded I-Ching chance engine; Cage's *Sonatas and Interludes* + *Music of Changes*), **`13184-heliacal`** (geolocation + local clock → NOAA solar-position ephemeris drives a scrubbable long-form circadian day-composition; Max Richter's *Sleep* long-form lineage). **Shipped `13168-preparedchance`** — the highest-surprise, strongest named reference (Cage), the most literal cash of jury #1 ("audio from OUTSIDE the piece" — here it's *you* playing, re-composed), and dead-on Karel's live-performance care (a playable MIDI/QWERTY instrument in the jury's own praised non-cosmic lineage, rosewindow / oraclequartet). Banked the other two to IDEAS §1137. **Next hooks:** the external-data modality is still thin and jury-praised (magnetos was a standout) — a live-air-quality "city breathing" piece and an ISS-overhead orbital sonification are both CORS-open/no-key candidates; and the three modalities here can each deepen (tiltbowl → true ambisonic HRTF room; heliacal → a full year's light, not just a day). **NOT ear/device-verified** (headless — no MIDI, no speakers): whether the prepared-string presets sit musically and the chance re-composition feels like a collaborator want Karel's device; the seeded muted demo (a modal phrase run silently through the oracle, alive on the first painted frame) is the review stand-in.

## 2026-08-14 · §1136 — DEEP (ledger 1132 D · 1133 W · 1134 D · 1135 W → 1136 DEEP): **agent/particle-based self-organizing morphogenesis is the live 2026 frontier**, and the lab had NEVER built a morphogenesis piece — so this cycle shipped its first.

15-min dive (WebSearch + WebFetch, dates checked). The freshest, most surprising thread: **[Neural Particle Automata: Learning Self-Organizing Particle Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.16096)** (arXiv:2601.16096, **Jan 2026**) replaces the grid-based perception of Neural Cellular Automata with *differentiable particle (SPH) operators* — i.e. the morphogenesis frontier is explicitly moving from grids to **many tiny local agents with no global plan, a whole form emerging** (it still demonstrates the classic NCA hallmarks: robustness, regeneration). Corroborated by **[Neural Cellular Automata: From Cells to Pixels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.22899)** (arXiv:2506.22899, **Jan 2026**), which re-anchors the whole field on **Turing's 1952 reaction-diffusion model of morphogenesis** — "simple hand-crafted local rules over time yield intricate global patterns." Why surprising for us: the dream lab has ~13,000 prototypes but a grep audit (`physarum|slime.mold`, `reaction.diffusion|gray.scott`, `differential.growth|space.colonization`) found **zero** — the entire *morphogenesis / self-organizing growth* family (physarum, Gray-Scott reaction-diffusion, space-colonization venation) was untouched, despite being exactly the TouchDesigner/Houdini-lineage the mandate keeps pointing at (Sage Jenson physarum, Entagma differential growth). It is also the cleanest antidote to the jury-2026-08-14 "cosmic self-playing-shader monoculture": a growth system is a *living structure* driven by real audio from outside the piece, not a hand-painted field of light reading a self-drone. **Became a prototype this cycle:** ONE concept "sound grows a living form" raced across 3 hand-crafted-agent morphogenesis techniques (the honest ancestors of the learned NPA/NCA line) — **physarum agent transport network** (shipped `13104-physarumreef`), **Gray-Scott reaction-diffusion** (banked `13120-turingbloom`), **space-colonization venation** (banked `13136-venation`) — each with real dropped-file/mic audio shaping the growth. Chain visible: today's dive → today's build (case a). **Next hooks:** a genuinely *learned* NCA (a tiny hand-rolled differentiable update, no libs — the ambition-#1 "trained model in the browser" lane) that grows a target texture *to* the music; a physarum whose trail field is seeded from Karel's real Path-piano spectral flux.

## 2026-08-14 · §1135 — WIDE (ledger 1131 W · 1132 D · 1133 W · 1134 DEEP → 1135 WIDE): the phase-vocoder **spectral freeze** is a *living* real-time practice in 2026 (KVR "spectral accumulation" thread; the recreational-signal-processing "BlueNote's real-time phase vocoder" writeup; Grainfreeze), and the honest craft in it is **identity phase-locking** — locking each spectral peak's neighbour bins to the peak's phase so a held frame rings without the metallic buzz

15-min dive (WebSearch, dates checked; ledger says WIDE, and the fresh [JURY 2026-08-14](JURY.md) #6 "extend recallorbit" was already cashed by §1134 `12976-dreammedley` → this cycle takes the jury's *other* loud provocation instead). **Two live threads.** (1) The **phase vocoder spectral freeze** is not a museum algorithm — it's actively re-implemented in 2026 real-time tools ([KVR "spectral accumulation using phase vocoder"](https://www.kvraudio.com/forum/viewtopic.php?t=489310); [BlueNote's real-time phase vocoder, recreationalsignalprocessing substack](https://recreationalsignalprocessing.substack.com/p/bluenotes-real-time-phase-vocoder); [PhaseVocoderJS with identity phase-locking](https://github.com/echo66/PhaseVocoderJS); Grainfreeze VST). A freeze *repeatedly IFFTs the same magnitude frame, advancing each bin's phase by its frozen per-hop increment*; the quality lever the current writeups stress is **identity phase-locking** (Laroche & Dolson 1999) — lock a peak's region-of-influence bins to the peak phase so partials stay coherent and the held sound is a still, luminous chord rather than a buzz. (2) Corroborating the "structure-as-image" side I *rejected* this cycle: [arXiv music-structure work](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.19816) confirms **self-similarity matrix / recurrence plots** (Foote 1999) remain the core of music-structure segmentation — but a grep found the lab already owns this verbatim (`5384-cartograph`, `777-song-architecture`), so it is a re-tread, not a first. **Honest freshness note:** Laroche & Dolson / Foote are foundational (1999); the *living* layer is the 2026 real-time-freeze re-implementations + the current SSM-structure papers — flagged honestly, same browser-feasible-register standard recent dives use. **Chains → today's WIDE (case a).** ONE research thread (real-time spectral freeze) seeded 3 divergent explorers, each dodging every jury-banned tag (self-drone input · full-screen WebGL2 frag shader · analyser-self-drone · cosmic/tunnel-of-light · volumetric ray-march) via a different INPUT × TECHNIQUE × PALETTE: **`13040-spectralhold`** (mic-PRIMARY phase-vocoder identity-phase-locked freeze → stack your own frozen breaths into a self-choir; near-black austere Canvas2D — the research headliner + the most literal cash of jury #1 "kill the self-drone, audio from OUTSIDE" and #5 "audio-first/non-screen"), **`13056-recurrence`** (real recorded track → chroma self-similarity matrix, click a cell to hear the rhyme; Ikeda-clinical), **`13072-flowfield`** (webcam dense optical flow → granular/Karplus instrument; embodied violet-ink trails). **Shipped `13040-spectralhold`** (WIDE-winner) — the least duplicative of the three and the strongest cash of the jury's loudest ask (mic as PRIMARY instrument, nothing sounds until you freeze → it deliberately does NOT "work muted on a phone," which is the point of provocation #1). Banked the other two to IDEAS §1135, each with an honest prior-art caveat (recurrence ≈ cartograph; flowfield's optical-flow→music predates it in `221-optical-flow-music`/`1160-body-choir`). Curated to 1. **NOT ear/device-verified** (headless — no mic/speakers): whether the frozen-frame resynthesis is click-free and the stacked freezes sit as a pleasant chord on a real mic+speaker wants Karel's device; a silent fallback vowel-synth stands in so the page is never dead.

## 2026-08-14 · §1134 — DEEP (jury-#6-ordered extend of the loved 4/5 `11376-recallorbit`): the reservoir-computing frontier is now **attention over a finite set of memory slots** — the *Echo State Transformer* — which is exactly the architecture for "a mind that holds many of the melodies you sang and dreams in the space between them"

15-min dive (WebSearch + WebFetch, dates checked; ledger 1131 W · 1132 D · 1133 W → **1134 DEEP** by strict alternation, AND the fresh **[JURY 2026-08-14](JURY.md)** #6 EXPLICITLY orders it: "Extend recallorbit, don't clone it… a cycle-3 that lets you *sing your own melody in* and watch the trained readout reproduce it exactly, then dream it apart, honors the real multi-cycle commitment"). **The live thread:** the reservoir-computing line the lab is already walking (`10984-echofold` fixed-random readouts → `11376-recallorbit` one ridge-trained readout, exact recall + dream) has a natural next rung in the current literature — the **[Echo State Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.02917)** (Baldini et al.), which replaces global self-attention with a bank of **multiple parallel reservoirs used as a fixed-size working memory** and runs **attention only over those finite memory slots** (linear-complexity, biologically-motivated working memory). Corroborating the interpolation half: **[MusicVAE's 2-D latent palette](https://arxiv.org/abs/2308.05496)** (Roberts, Engel et al.) established the human-facing pattern of *decoding interpolated points between corner melodies*, and the current singing-voice-synthesis frontier ([VocalRender, arXiv:2607.27768, July 2026](https://arxiv.org/html/2607.27768)) keeps real-time, score-native voice reproduction in active development. **Grep-audit BEFORE billing:** the lab owns a genuine trained single-readout reservoir (`11376-recallorbit`) and single-phrase latent-walk pieces (banked `9112-latentwalk`), but a grep of every README for `interpolat|morph between|memory map|latent space|repertoire` returns only pitch-detection and geometry uses — **NOTHING holds MULTIPLE of your sung melodies on one reservoir and lets you navigate the space between them.** So NO candidate bills a false grep-0 #1 (ESN, ridge readout, dream knob all pre-exist in recallorbit); the fresh value — and the jury #6 ask — is **many trained readouts sharing one reservoir basis, blended by attention**, which the Echo State Transformer names as an architecture. **Key implementation insight that made all three lanes cheap + genuine:** because a clock-driven reservoir's state sequence `X` is identical for every taught phrase, `XᵀX+λI` is a property of the *reservoir* not the melody → Cholesky-factored ONCE, each phrase a single back-substitution, and (readout linear in state) a convex blend of readouts is a convex blend of melodies at every phase — so the between-space is real musical hybrid, not mush. **Chains → today's DEEP (case a):** ONE concept — *a reservoir mind holds every melody you sing to it and lets you dream in the latent space between your own songs* — raced 3 ways: **`12976-dreammedley`** (a 5-min+ self-navigating long-form dream through your memories — the #4 long-form-state lane, SHIPPED), **`12960-memoryfold`** (a MusicVAE-style 2-D memory pad you drag to blend your songs — the explorable-instrument lane), **`12992-mirrorduet`** (a canon partner that attends over your past selves — the literal Echo State Transformer made audible). Shipped the long-form lane because JURY 2026-08-14's core complaint is a *flattened ceiling* and it names long-form-with-state as the thin untouched category; the other two banked to IDEAS §1134. **NOT ear/device-verified** (headless — no speakers): each carries a seeded deterministic auto-dream that trains + navigates with zero mic so the muted 06:30 review still watches the memory-arc build.

## 2026-08-14 · §1132 — DEEP (jury-ordered spatial/embodied): interactive movement sonification is a LIVING 2026 practice built on *loops* (CHI 2026 hip-hop-dance study) — the fresh embodied verb the lab hasn't done is a room that RECORDS and LOOPS your moving body into a spatial canon of your past selves

15-min dive (WebSearch, dates checked; ledger 1128 D · 1129 W · 1130 D · 1131 W → **1132 DEEP** by strict alternation, AND the fresh **[JURY 2026-08-14](JURY.md)** #4 explicitly orders "spend a DEEP cycle on spatial / embodied — the mandate's top thin category, untouched"). **The live thread:** interactive *movement sonification* is a current, actively-published practice, and the 2026 work centers **loops** — [**"Designing Interactive Movement Sonification for Hip-Hop Dance"** (CHI 2026, DOI 10.1145/3772318.3790703)](https://dl.acm.org/doi/full/10.1145/3772318.3790703) frames the instrument as a **perception–action loop**: dancers make rapid movement decisions inside interactive sound loops, and "embodied listening" is the goal — the body is not a trigger, it's the composer. Corroborated by [**"Fluid Body: An Adaptive Embodied Sonification System for Cross-Cultural Performance"** (Springer 2026)](https://link.springer.com/chapter/10.1007/978-3-032-24350-8_11) (posture-classifier + regression → granular synthesis) and low-latency HRTF motion→sound coupling (<40 ms) as the spatial-audio norm. **Grep-audit BEFORE billing (jury #5):** the lab's embodied lane is well-populated — `677-presence-field` (MediaPipe-pose → per-joint PannerNode in a 3-D room), `1160-body-choir` (optical-flow → choir), `1590-body-mirror` (hands → spatial pentatonic), `5096-theurgy` (hands → plasma) — all **live-only**: your body plays sound *right now* and the room is silent again the instant you stop. **NONE of them RECORD + LOOP + LAYER the body.** So NO candidate bills a grep-0 #1 (body-tracking + HRTF + MediaPipe + looping all pre-exist individually); the fresh value — and the jury's "concentrate the ambition / genuine long-form with state" ask — is the **combination into an accumulating spatial body-canon**: minute 5 is a room full of your looping past selves, minute 1 was empty. **Chains → today's DEEP (case a):** ONE concept — *a room that records and loops your moving body into a spatial canon of your past selves; the empty room is silent, you fill it only by moving* — raced 3 ways: **`11840-bodyloom`** (MediaPipe-pose skeleton → per-gesture looping HRTF voice-cluster; richest read, CDN-dependent), **`11856-shadecanon`** (model-free silhouette extremal-points, no network → looping spatial voices, Canvas2D room; the robust always-works lane), **`11872-motionwell`** (optical-flow motion-energy → recorded spatial GRAIN-cloud loops that decay over minutes; the textural long-form lane). Directly cashes jury #1 (audio comes from your MOTION, not a self-drone — an empty still room makes NO sound), #4 (embodied/spatial DEEP), and the categorical "long-form generative with state/memory/evolution." **NOT device-verified** (headless — no camera/GPU/speakers); each carries a seeded scripted demo-dancer that records + loops with zero camera so the muted 06:30 review still watches the canon build.



## 2026-08-14 · §1131 — WIDE: WebGPU-compute→compositor is now live VJ tooling (SIGGRAPH Real-Time Live! 2026 + Ghost Arcade), and the peripheral-drift "Rotating Snakes" illusion is a strobe-SAFE way to make a static light-field appear to rotate/breathe with sound — a pure neuroscience-of-perception altered-state hook

15-min dive (WebSearch, dates checked; ledger 1128 D · 1129 W · 1130 D → **1131 WIDE** by strict alternation, which §1130 MORNING/STATE pre-announced). **Two threads, both browser-feasible.** (1) **[SIGGRAPH Real-Time Live! 2026](https://gamedev.net/news/4682-watch-it-work-real-time-live-2026-brings-emerging-technology-to-the-stage-in-la/)** (LA, Jul 21 2026) put real-time interactive graphics back on stage, and the [WebGPU generative-art community](https://www.webgpu.com/tag/generative-art/) + **[Ghost Arcade](https://ghostarcade.live/)** now run **WebGPU compute shaders zero-copy from compute output straight into the compositor** — the current-practice signal that GPU-compute→display is shipping VJ tooling, not a research demo (the lab's WebGPU-compute line, flagged 0× in the last-15 jury window, is worth reclaiming on a real-device slot). (2) The **peripheral-drift illusion** — Akiyoshi Kitaoka's *[Rotating Snakes](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Peripheral_drift_illusion)* / [Faubert & Herbert (1999)](https://www.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/pmc/articles/PMC3240464/) — is a motion illusion produced by a **static** sawtooth-luminance grating in peripheral vision (asymmetric dark→gray→white→gray order), with recent [V1/MT motion-energy modelling](https://www.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/pmc/articles/PMC3084698/) attributing the illusory rotation to luminance-dependent response latency in direction-selective cells. This is exactly the VISIONARY.md "neuroscience of perception" mandate AND a **strobe-safety win by construction** — the field never flashes; the illusory motion lives in static luminance order, so modulating ring contrast/asymmetry with sound changes the *perceived* rotation speed with zero real flicker. **Honest freshness note:** Kitaoka/Faubert are foundational (1999/2003); the fresh layer is the Jul-2026 Real-Time Live! + Ghost Arcade WebGPU-compositor practice and the recent motion-energy modelling — flagged honestly, same browser-feasible-register standard recent dives used. **Chains → today's WIDE (case a).** Three explorers, each cashing a different provocation via non-banned tags: **`11792-snakevoid`** (thread 2 — a static peripheral-drift light-field whose illusory rotation answers to a self-driving/mic-modulated bed; the research headliner + a genuine perception-science piece), **`11808-stillpoint`** (the screen-bias / non-screen categorical gap — an audio-FIRST meditative void from Karel's real Path piano: granular time-stretch + HRTF slow-orbit + isochronic entrainment behind a near-blank CSS breathing disc), **`11824-honeyveil`** (jury #1 real GPU-3-D geometry — tilt-steered flythrough of a hyperbolic honeycomb, Klüver's honeycomb form-constant, cells sonified as an inharmonic bell-lattice). ## 2026-08-14 · §1130 — DEEP: the oscilloscope-music / XY vector-synthesis tradition is a LIVING practice (Fenderson, macumbista, a 2026 GPU scope), and real-time in-browser pitch estimation is now cheap enough to LOCK a sung note into a stable Lissajous knot

15-min dive (WebSearch, dates checked; ledger 1127 W · 1128 D · 1129 W → **1130 DEEP** by strict alternation, which §1129 MORNING/STATE pre-announced + queued the perpetual RESURRECT-FIRST `drawnsound` as "promote as a DEEP: pitch-locked knots + transform-feedback filaments"). **Two threads, both browser-feasible, both sharpening the drawn-sound DEEP.** (1) The **oscilloscope-music / XY vector-synthesis** tradition is not a museum piece — it's actively developed: **[Jerobeam Fenderson's *Oscilloscope Music*](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Oscilloscope_Music)** (two audio channels steer an X-Y beam so sound *is* the image; Hansi Raber's OsciStudio), the **[macumbista *Vector Synthesis* toolkit](https://github.com/macumbista/vectorsynthesis)** (Derek Holzer — driving a beam/CRT/laser directly rather than a raster), and a current crop of WebGL XY-oscilloscope renderers with phosphor/bloom + a GPU-accelerated desktop "Phosphor" XY scope shipping through 2026 ([in-browser XY oscilloscopes map stereo channels to the two axes to draw Lissajous figures](https://cookingwithsound.com/an-xy-mode-oscilloscope-in-your-browser/)). Procedural per-vertex vector graphics modulated by live audio is a genuinely distinct output from the fragment-field raymarchers + GPU point clouds the lab has been shipping. (2) **Real-time pitch estimation** got cheap and self-supervised — **[PESTO: Real-Time Pitch Estimation with a Self-supervised Transposition-equivariant Objective (arXiv:2508.01488)](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2508.01488)** is a lightweight, low-latency single-note estimator — which is exactly what lets a sung note be *quantized to a simple integer ratio and LOCKED* into a stable Lissajous knot (a browser YIN-lite autocorrelation in the same spirit is enough). **Honest freshness note:** McLaren/Laposky/Fenderson + Lissajous are foundational; the *living* layer is the macumbista/OsciStudio/WebGL-scope practice + the 2026 GPU scope + PESTO (Aug-2025, still the current real-time-pitch reference) — flagged honestly, same browser-feasible-register standard recent dives used. **Chains → today's DEEP (case c — the build consumes IDEAS §1129 `drawnsound`, itself seeded by RESEARCH §1129's oscillographic-revival thread, now sharpened by these two threads).** DEEP finally cashes the **perpetual RESURRECT-FIRST `drawnsound`** (banked §1127 + §1129 — the §1126 `lumenorgan`→`rosewindow` precedent: a DEEP ships a 2×+-deferred bank by racing renderings + elevating with a named reference), racing ONE concept — *sing, and your voice is drawn as living light, the oscillographic drawn-sound tradition made a voice-played instrument* — across 3 renderings: **`11744-drawnsound`** (the faithful ~1,400-filament CPU streamline tangle), **`11760-filamentchoir`** (the massive ~24k-filament **GPU transform-feedback** version), **`11776-lissaknot`** (a pure **X-Y oscilloscope** where a **YIN-lite pitch estimate LOCKS** a held note into a clean integer-ratio Lissajous knot). Shipped **`11776-lissaknot`** — the biggest surprise + most legible "huh" (a held note *snaps* to a glowing knot), the most literal descendant of the named tradition, low device-risk standard additive lines (dodging the 5×-flagged unverifiable-GPU trap that banked filamentchoir), phone-*exercisable* (jury 2026-08-13 #2 sensor-reclaim — sing into the review phone), and it elevates drawnsound beyond its faithful base exactly as rosewindow elevated lumenorgan. ## 2026-08-14 · §1128 — DEEP: real-time volumetric clouds fake MULTIPLE scattering with a cheap multi-octave trick — the energy-conserving approximation that turns cloudveil's rim-lit single-scatter into a cloud that glows from DEEP inside

15-min dive (WebSearch, dates checked; ledger 1125 W · 1126 D · 1127 W → **1128 DEEP** by strict alternation, which §1127 MORNING/STATE pre-announced ("DEEP is due next fire" + queued item (4) "Deepen 11600")). **The finding:** the modern real-time volumetric-cloud pipeline does NOT compute true multiple scattering — it *approximates* it with **Sébastien Hillaire's multi-octave trick** ("Physically Based and Unified Volumetric Rendering in Frostbite"; resurveyed in the [May-2026 Cinevva overview of volumetric clouds in modern games](https://app.cinevva.com/blog/2026-05-04-volumetric-clouds-and-weather)): sum N octaves where octave i scales extinction by `aⁱ`, scattering contribution by `bⁱ`, and Henyey-Greenstein eccentricity by `cⁱ` (a≈b≈c≈0.5) — the low octaves keep the crisp forward-lit rim, the high octaves see almost no extinction so light leaks from deep inside as a broad near-shadowless glow, and the whole thing is energy-conserving and per-frame cheap. The frontier is actively pushing further into learned/precomputed transport — [**Real-time Neural Six-way Lightmaps** (arXiv:2604.03748, Apr 2026)](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2604.03748) precomputes six-directional light interaction through pre-simulated 3-D density fields, and [Environmental Volumetric Neural Shading of Clouds (ACM CGIT, 2026)](https://dl.acm.org/doi/10.1145/3820020) does per-frame neural cloud shading — both banked as future GPU-verifiable directions, too heavy for a headless single-pass shader today. **Honest audit (jury #5):** `multiple.?scatter|multi.?octave|temporal.?reproj` = grep-0 in the lab (the sole incidental hit was `246-spectral-splat`, unrelated); onset detection is broadly present (387 files) so the beat-surge is NOT billed a first. Freshness note: Hillaire's approximation is foundational (2015–2020) but the May-2026 Cinevva survey + the Apr-2026 six-way-lightmaps paper confirm it's the live re-surveyed frontier — flagged honestly, same browser-feasible-register standard recent dives used. **Chains → today's DEEP (case a — the winner IS this dive's implementation).** DEEP does exactly what jury #3 asked ("concentrate the ambition — take one *proven* first multi-cycle to 5/5; stop shipping fifteen honest mediums, ship one genuinely massive"): take cloudveil's **proven grep-0 first** (participating-media light transport) and make it massive via **multi-octave energy-conserving MULTIPLE scattering + a beat-locked camera that surges you forward on onsets + audio-chroma → sun-tint**, raced across 3 technical approaches of the SAME concept — **`11648-lightfold`** (single-pass, the faithful multi-octave-MS deepening of the open drift), **`11664-deepdrift`** (a 3-pass WebGL2 temporal-reprojection accumulation buffer — grep-0 rendering architecture — spending the cost across frames for band-free deep interiors), **`11680-corridor`** (the multi-octave MS lighting a path-guided **navigable tunnel** — cloudveil's open drift became a corridor you fly down toward the light, the literal NDE tunnel). Shipped **`11680-corridor`** — the biggest concept-leap (navigable geometry, not just better lighting), the most on-charter (literal near-death tunnel-of-light, AGENT.md §260), mechanic legible on a muted 06:30 phone, single-pass = low device risk. ## 2026-08-14 · §1127 — WIDE: raymarching finally grew a THIRD dimension of light — real *participating-media* light transport (Beer-Lambert + Henyey-Greenstein) is now browser-real-time, so a cloud of light can actually SCATTER, not just be an SDF surface

15-min dive (WebSearch, dates checked; ledger 1124 D · 1125 W · 1126 D → **1127 WIDE** by strict alternation). **The finding:** the browser raymarching frontier has moved from *surface* SDFs to *volumetric participating media* — ray-marching a 3-D density field and accumulating light with **Beer-Lambert absorption** (`transmittance *= exp(-density·σ·ds)`) and an **anisotropic Henyey-Greenstein phase function** for forward light-scatter, i.e. real cloud/fog light transport rather than a lit surface. [Maxime Heckel's "Real-time dreamy Cloudscapes with Volumetric Raymarching"](https://blog.maximeheckel.com/posts/real-time-cloudscapes-with-volumetric-raymarching/) is the reference implementation, and pure-WebGPU volumetric-cloud compute shaders ([joshbrew/webgpu_realtime_clouds](https://github.com/joshbrew/webgpu_realtime_clouds)) confirm it runs at frame-rate now. The other genuinely-fresh on-theme find was [**LUMIA: A Handheld Vision-to-Music System for Real-Time, Embodied Composition** (arXiv:2512.17228, Dec 2025)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.17228) — camera→music embodied composition — banked for a future camera cycle (camera is recently used: veilbreak, orbithall). **Honest audit (jury #5, "grep the first before you bill it"):** `raymarch` appears in **65** lab files — but they are all SDF *surfaces*; `henyey|greenstein|beer.?lambert|participating.media|volumetric.scatter` = **grep-0**. So true volumetric light-transport IS a genuine grep-0 #1 technique (physarum and chladni, my other two candidate techniques, both greped POSITIVE — `1089-cosmic-connectome`/`1916-slime-cantor` and `7032-chladni`/`1628-chladni-choir` — and were rejected). **Chains → today's WIDE (case a — one of three explorers implements this dive's hook).** WIDE fans three divergent explorers across distinct substrates × inputs × techniques × palettes, all off every live ban: **`11600-cloudveil`** (implements THIS dive — **audio-file/Path-piano** → **WebGL2-fragment volumetric raymarch** with Beer-Lambert + Henyey-Greenstein scattering, warm dawn-gold cloud-of-light toward a white sun, cosmic-ambient/NDE-tunnel — the grep-0 #1), **`11616-glowdrift`** (**self-driving long-form generative** → **WebGPU-compute** spore-field over a **Voss-McCartney 1/f** process driving harmony AND flow so it's audibly different at minute 5, bioluminescent verdant — the jury's #3 "concentrate ambition / go long-form"), **`11632-drawnsound`** (**live mic** voice → **WebGL2 additive line/point vector-graphics** in the **McLaren/Laposky drawn-sound** oscillographic tradition, ivory-on-indigo INTENSE — the jury's #2 sensor-reclaim, phone-exercisable). ## 2026-08-14 · §1126 — DEEP: a chord already HAS a colour — Olivier Messiaen SAW specific colours for chords and assigned a dominant colour to every transposition of his modes, a real, portable synesthetic system to make a playable instrument of light

15-min dive (WebSearch, dates checked; ledger 1122 D · 1123 W · 1124 D · 1125 W → **1126 DEEP** by strict alternation, which the §1125 MORNING pre-announced by asking Karel outright: *"lumenorgan has banked 3× — next cycle's DEEP promotes it into a real, playable MIDI light-organ."*). **The finding:** the deepest, most *credibly-authored* mapping from harmony to colour is not a generative-AI guess — it is **Olivier Messiaen's sound-colour synaesthesia**, a documented, systematic correspondence a composer built over a lifetime. Messiaen genuinely *saw* colours when he heard chords and, in his treatise, **assigns a dominant colour (or set) to each transposition of his [Modes of Limited Transposition](https://medium.com/@newmusiccomposer/messiaens-modes-of-limited-transposition-my-personal-take-a37f829edd81)** — e.g. Mode 2 at its first transposition = gradations of **blue-violet** (his favourite), other modes/transpositions run through orange-gold, green, red, purple ([MTO / Benitez essay on colour↔sound structure](https://www.mtosmt.org/issues/mto.02.8.2/mto.02.8.2.benitez_essay.html)); the modes' symmetry means each has a fixed harmonic *and chromatic* identity. Modern work frames the same bridge computationally — **[musicolors (arXiv:2503.14220, Mar 2025)](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2503.14220)** bridges sound↔visuals for synaesthetic rendering, and **["Rainbows in my ears" (PMC12673365, 2025)](https://www.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/pmc/articles/PMC12673365/)** shows synaesthetic colour is driven by pitch height, timbre, **chord**, and key — the exact inputs a keyboard emits. **The hook:** a **playable instrument of light** where each note/chord blooms *its true Messiaen colour* and overlapping notes **additively fuse** (via `mix-blend-mode:screen`) into the blended colour of the chord — so a musician literally plays the composer's own synaesthesia, on a **pure CSS/DOM light-field** (zero canvas/WebGL). **Honest audit (jury #5, "grep the first before you bill it"):** Web MIDI pre-exists in the lab (44 files) and the CSS/DOM-compositor substrate pre-exists (`7592-floatwell` §1040, and the 3×-banked `lumenorgan`), so this is **NOT a grep-0 #1** — BUT **Messiaen sound-colour mapping is grep-0 in the lab (0 hits)**, so the fresh value is the *named-reference port* (jury #3 — "a living technique ported, not a dead-inventor name-drop": Messiaen's colour system is exactly a living, documented practice) + a genuinely **playable, touch-exercisable** instrument (the 3×-deferral reason — MIDI unshowable on a phone — is killed by adding an on-screen TOUCH keyboard). Freshness note: Messiaen is foundational (not last-30-days), the computational bridges are 2025; flagged honestly, consistent with the browser-feasible-register standard the last several dives used. **Chains → today's DEEP (case a — implement this dive's hook).** DEEP races ONE concept — *an instrument of light you can PLAY, where every note is its true Messiaen colour and chords fuse into one living stained-glass field* — across three genuinely-different layouts/interaction models of the SAME playable CSS-compositor + Messiaen-colour engine (MIDI + on-screen touch + computer-keys; self-playing modal chorale self-demo so a muted phone sees+hears it in ~1s): **`11552-lumenorgan`** (the grid stained-glass compositor, promoted — 12×5 additive glow grid, chord-fusion), **`11568-rosewindow`** (a POLAR rose-window MANDALA — pitch-class = angle, octave = radius; playing draws a living cathedral rose that IS a visionary mandala — marries the PRIMARY charter), **`11584-chromafield`** (a breathing horizon/aurora light-field where held chords sustain as one fused Messiaen colour-wash — the cosmic-ambient lane). Off every live ban (input **MIDI+touch**, 0× recent & the jury's #2 sensor-reclaim, touch = phone-exercisable · output **CSS/DOM-compositor**, 0× in last 10, the freshest substrate & the jury's #1 "stop the Canvas2D/SVG/three.js rut" · technique **Messiaen sound-colour + additive chord-fusion**, grep-0 · palette **multi-hue Messiaen jewel**, off the camped cyan-cosmic AND violet-on-black AND warm-molten).
