## 2026-08-13 · §1123 — WIDE: DIVERGENCE-FREE CURL NOISE is the live 2026 audio-reactive-GPU move — turbulence with no fluid solve, so a music track can *condense form out of pure noise* and dissolve it back

15-min dive (WebSearch, dates checked; ledger 1120 DEEP · 1121 WIDE · 1122 DEEP → **1123 WIDE** by strict alternation). **The finding:** the current browser way to make ~100k particles swirl audio-reactively is **curl-noise flow-field advection on a WebGPU compute pipeline** — the curl of a procedural-noise *potential* field, which is **mathematically divergence-free** (no sources, no sinks: particles neither clump nor scatter), so you get endlessly organic turbulence with **NO PDE/pressure solve** — it is explicitly *not* a fluid/physics sim, it's a closed-form vector field you sample per particle. Live examples this year: Robert Borghesi's **[ASTRODITHER](https://medium.com/@midnightdemise123/creating-chaotic-flow-fields-with-gpgpu-in-react-three-fiber-f9aad608c534)** (three.js-WebGPU/TSL, curl-noise + bloom + time-warped signal noise) and the reusable **[three.js-blocks `CurlNoise` WebGPU/WebGL module](https://www.threejs-blocks.com/docs/module-CurlNoise)** / [takumifukasawa's WebGPU curl-noise compute demo](https://github.com/takumifukasawa/webgpu-particle-compute-shader-curl-noise-demo) — but **none of that literature wires the flow to a pianist's real recorded music.** That gap is the hook: drive curl-scale + a coherence/attractor pull from live spectral energy so loud passages **condense** the veil into form and quiet ones let it **dissolve** to drifting light. **Honest audit (jury provocation #5, "grep the first before you bill it"):** WebGPU compute + audio-reactive particle clouds both pre-exist in the lab (`1348-prism-cortex`, `4856-orrery`, `243-spectral-cloud`, `11312-voxbloom`), so this is NOT a grep-0 #1 — the novelty is the *curl-noise substrate* + the condense/dissolve gesture, billed as #2+#5. **Chains → today's WIDE.** This dive's case-(a) implementation was built as one of three divergent explorers — **`11440-flowveil`** (audio-file → WebGPU curl-noise aurora, three.js `Points` fallback, verdant teal↔gold) — and **banked to IDEAS §1123** (its headline WebGPU path can't be device-verified headless; resurrect once GPU-verifiable, or as a DEEP that hardens the fallback into a first-class view). The WIDE **winner** was instead the sensor-reclaim lane the *live* jury's most-open provocation (#2, reclaim a live sensor) demanded — `11408-tiltglide` (device-tilt), itself a §1121-research-seeded banked idea (case c). The dive still logged, the case-(a) build banked, the chain visible.

## 2026-08-13 · §1122 — DEEP: TRAINED READOUTS turn a reservoir from a *drifter* into a *recaller* — feed it raw audio, train only the linear readout, and it sings your phrase back EXACTLY (then dissolves to dream)

15-min dive (WebSearch, dates checked; ledger 1119 W · 1120 DEEP · 1121 WIDE → **1122 DEEP** by strict alternation, and the **2026-08-13 jury's provocation #3** makes DEEP a near-direct order: *"Concentrate the ambition — take one FIRST multi-cycle to 5/5 … spend a DEEP cycle deepening a proven first — … echofold wants trained readouts for exact transposition."*). **The finding:** the live-2026 reservoir-computing register has moved to **feature-free, raw-audio reservoirs whose ONLY trained part is a one-shot linear readout** — **[Direct Raw Audio Signal Processing via Reservoir Computing: An Investigation into "Feature-Free" Architectures (arXiv:2606.21335, Jun 2026)](https://arxiv.org/html/2606.21335)** feeds raw acoustic waveforms straight into a fixed reservoir and trains only the readout layer, bypassing the pre-processing bottleneck; **[Bridging Biological Hearing and Neuromorphic Computing: End-to-End Time-Domain Audio Signal Processing with Reservoir Computing (arXiv:2603.24283, Mar 2026)](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2603.24283)** frames the same low-power, low-latency edge-audio reservoir. Both confirm: a reservoir is a cheap, real-time audio machine whose learning is a **single ridge-regression readout fit** — no backprop, no ML libs. **Why it matters for the lab:** `10984-echofold` (§1114 — the 08-13 jury's 4/5 reservoir standout) used **FIXED RANDOM readouts by design**, so it can only ever *drift*; it can't reproduce. ## 2026-08-13 · §1120 — DEEP: the Direct-to-Reverberant Ratio (DRR) is the psychoacoustic cue for *distance* — the exact mechanism to take orbitroom's HRTF room from a flat pan to a room with felt depth

15-min dive (WebSearch, dates checked; ledger 1117 W · 1118 DEEP · 1119 WIDE → **1120 DEEP** by strict alternation, and the **new 2026-08-13 jury** — replacing the 08-12 one — makes this cycle nearly a direct order: provocation **#3** "spend a DEEP cycle deepening a *proven first* — **orbitroom's HRTF room wants per-source distance reverb**"; **#1** "rest Canvas2D AND SVG-DOM, ship a GPU-geometry piece (three.js / WebGPU)"; **#2** "reclaim a live sensor — camera is rested." A single DEEP concept discharges all three.). **The finding:** across the spatial-audio literature, a listener's ability to judge how *far* a source is in an enclosed space is governed not by loudness alone but by the **Direct-to-Reverberant Ratio (DRR)** — the level of the direct (dry) sound relative to the reverberant (wet) field. A near source is direct-dominated (high DRR: loud, dry, bright early reflections); a far source is reverb-dominated (low DRR: quieter direct, longer wetter tail, darker). Sources: **[Past, Present, and Future of Spatial Audio and Room Acoustics — survey, arXiv:2503.12948](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2503.12948)** (frames DRR as the core distance cue and convolution/room-model reverb as its renderer) + **[Perceptual implications of simplifying geometrical-acoustics models for Ambisonics-based binaural reverberation, arXiv:2411.10375](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2411.10375)** (how much room-model fidelity actually matters perceptually — you can synthesize a convincing distance cue cheaply) + the 2026 live register of **personalized HRTF** ([HRTFformer, arXiv:2510.01891](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2510.01891); Apple ear-scan Personalized Spatial Audio) which keeps binaural rendering the hot axis. **Why this matters for the lab:** `10808-orbitroom` (the 08-13 jury's headline standout — the lab's first embodied body→HRTF spatial-audio room) explicitly banked its own next-cycle wish: *"give each source its own reverb send whose pre-delay and level track its distance to the listener, so stepping deeper into the ring changes each voice's near/far timbre, not just its pan."* DRR is the psychoacoustic name for exactly that. **Chains → today's DEEP (case b — continue a proven multi-cycle first).** DEEP raced ONE concept — *walk into a binaural room and FEEL its depth: each voice goes dry-and-bright as you approach, wet-and-washed as you recede* — across three renderings of the SAME engine (silhouette-centroid camera → 3-D listener position · per-source HRTF `PannerNode` · **per-source distance reverb where the wet/dry mix = the DRR**), all in **three.js 3-D** (off the jury-banned Canvas2D/SVG), camera-body input (off the banned pointer/keyboard desk-input), cool-aqueous palette (off the banned violet-on-black): **`11272-depthroom`** (first-person *inside* the hall, ConvolverNode per-source reverb + distance fog), **`11288-orbithall`** (third-person orbital — a live tether to each source *draws* its DRR so you SEE your spatialization, feedback-delay-network room), **`11304-nearfar`** (reductive 3-source corridor — the near/far timbre morph dialed to the max, the clearest single-idea read). Ambition honest **#2 (≥4 subsystems: silhouette CV + centroid/area tracking + HRTF field + per-source DRR distance-reverb + three.js 3-D scene + multi-voice synth) + #3 (HRTF/Omnitone binaural + DRR room-reverb, a living web-spatial-audio technique ported) + #4 (multi-cycle — cycle 2 of orbitroom's arc, the deepening it scoped) + #5 (this dive)** — deliberately **NOT** a faked #1 (HRTF/PannerNode is one of the most-worn techniques in the lab per the §1115 audit: hrtf/binaural/pannernode = 96/39/61 files — the value is the *DRR distance subsystem* + the 3-D room, not a first). Curate to 1; bank the other two to IDEAS §1120.

## 2026-08-13 · §1118 — THE ORACLE THAT IMPROVISES YOU BACK: the Factor Oracle (OMax) as a grep-0 machine-improviser — porting a *living* technique instead of name-dropping a dead inventor

15-min dive (WebSearch, dates checked; ledger 1114 DEEP · 1115 WIDE · 1116 DEEP · 1117 WIDE → **1118 DEEP** by strict alternation, and the 2026-08-12 jury's provocations #2 ["make the next DEEP a real #1 or #4"] + #3 ["criterion #3 has rotted into decoration — cite a *living technique you actually port*, not a dead-inventor name-drop"] are the exact order this cycle discharges). **The live 2026 register is human↔AI musical co-performance** — [Towards Real-Time Human–AI Musical Co-Performance (arXiv:2604.07612, Apr 2026)](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2604.07612) pairs a **latent-diffusion accompanist** with Max/MSP, and [The Ghost in the Keys: a Disklavier demo for Human-AI Musical Co-Creativity (arXiv:2511.01663)](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2511.01663) drives a player-piano — but every 2026 co-performer is a heavyweight GPU/offline model, **un-portable to a browser art gallery**. **The portable technique that delivers the SAME "a machine re-improvises me in real time" verb is the classic [Factor Oracle](https://hal.science/hal-01161221/document) (Assayag & Dubnov, "Using Factor Oracles for Machine Improvisation," 2004; the engine of IRCAM's OMax / Somax2 / ImproteK, still actively performed).** A Factor Oracle is an **online-built suffix-automaton** over a symbolized melody: states chained by a labelled forward spine, plus **suffix links** that connect every state to the earliest point sharing its longest-repeated-suffix context. Generation is a walk that mostly moves forward but, at a suffix link, JUMPS to a different region sharing the same recent context — yielding recombinations that are **locally coherent yet globally novel and non-repeating** (a genuine long-form state: different at minute 5 than minute 1). **Codebase audit → genuine grep-0 FIRST:** `suffix.?automat|suffix.?link`=**0**, and the only `factor.?oracle`/`omax` hits are a *deepen-note* in `285-mosaic-listener/README.md` (the path it explicitly never built) and a false-positive `oMax` variable in `954-sync-bloom`. The lab HAS worn *machine-improviser* siblings — `717-duet-mirror` (variable-order **Markov** style-learner), `718-duet-paths`/`285-mosaic-listener` (**concatenative/CataRT** corpus retrieval) — but the Factor Oracle is a **categorically different engine** (a deterministic suffix-automaton, not a probabilistic Markov chain and not corpus retrieval) and has never been built. So it clears the floor honestly via **#1 (grep-0 technique) + #3 (OMax/Assayag–Dubnov, a living technique genuinely ported, not a dead-inventor decoration) + #2 (≥3 subsystems)** — the precise thing the jury demanded. **Chains → today's DEEP (case a).** DEEP raced ONE concept — *feed it a melody and watch it improvise you back forever* — across three renderings of the SAME faithful Factor Oracle engine (online add_letter with correct transitions + suffix links + lrs-gated jumps; self-demo from a seeded modal head via `mulberry32`, or drop a WAV/MP3 so it learns and re-improvises your real piano): **`11144-oraclon`** (Canvas2D — the *automaton itself* as a living node-link graph; you SEE the read-head take each suffix-link jump), **`11160-recombinant`** (SVG-DOM piano-roll river where every recombination draws a glowing arc back to the fragment it borrowed), **`11176-oraclequartet`** (a self-playing jazz trio — FO solo + walking bass + comping, swung — the jury's named-absent *jazz* register + Karel's journey-engine-alternative directive). Off every live jury ban (no WebGL2-fragment-quad · no physics-sim family [PDE/CA/mass-spring/mechanism] · no warm-molten · no mic/tilt; and off three.js, just used in §1116): outputs {Canvas2D-graph / SVG-DOM-river / Canvas2D-noir}, palettes {high-contrast phosphor / cool indigo / jazz-noir cool}, input {audio-file + seeded self-demo}. Curate to 1; bank the other two to IDEAS §1118.

## 2026-08-13 · §1117 — REAL-TIME LIVE-MUSIC AGENTS & NETWORKED PERFORMANCE: the 2026 "embodied co-performer" frontier, and the control-event pattern that makes a multi-participant instrument feasible in-browser

15-min dive (WebSearch, dates checked; ledger 1113 W · 1114 DEEP · 1115 WIDE · 1116 DEEP → **1117 WIDE** by strict alternation, which the ambition mandate's WIDE-mode call — "3 distinct briefs, different tags each" — reinforces). **The live 2026 arXiv register is the real-time human↔AI live-music agent** — the field is explicitly pivoting music AI from offline "auxiliary generation tools" to **embodied co-performers that play alongside humans at conversational latency**: **[LK_Jam (arXiv:2606.21018, Jun 2026)](https://arxiv.org/html/2606.21018)** — a real-time interactive system on a Role-Aware GRU whose thesis is *turn-taking* — **["Real-Time Language Model Jamming" (arXiv:2606.11886, Jun 2026)](https://arxiv.org/html/2606.11886v1)** naming the core tension as *generative quality × interactive responsiveness × the physical constraints of computation and networking*, plus **["Towards Real-Time Human–AI Musical Co-Performance" (arXiv:2604.07612, Apr 2026)](https://arxiv.org/html/2604.07612v1)** (latent-diffusion accompaniment, consistency-distilled to 5.4× faster for real-time). **The genuinely portable finding for THIS lab (a browser art gallery, not an ML shop) turned out to be the *networking* half, not the model half:** the surveyed networked-music practice (Cornelljam band-app; beatshifting) converges on one pattern — **don't stream audio; transmit only lightweight control/timing events and have each client synthesize the sound LOCALLY** — which is what makes sub-50ms multi-participant performance feasible in a browser with no media server. That pattern is the key that unlocks the jury-2026-08-12's **single most-named unclaimed lever: multi-user, 0× for 15+ cycles** (its provocations #1/#2). **Chains → today's WIDE fan (case c — the third lane consumes this research-derived pattern):** three divergent explorers, each clearing the floor via DIFFERENT tags, off every live jury ban (no WebGL2-quad · no physics-sim family · no warm-molten · no mic/tilt · off the over-rep SVG-DOM/pointer/cosmic-ambient): **Lane A `11096-consort`** — a distributed traveling **canon across browser tabs** (control-events over `BroadcastChannel`, local per-client synthesis, a phrase relayed tab-to-tab as a living round; keyboard+cross-tab · Canvas2D · ceremonial) — the multi-user lever made real, this dive's direct hook. **Lane B `11112-abyssglow`** — a **Runions (2005/2007) space-colonization / venation growth** driven by piano onsets/RMS/centroid into a deep-sea bioluminescent bloom (audio-file+self-demo · three.js scene-graph geometry · verdant — a named living gen-art algorithm honestly ported, #3). **Lane C `11128-dropforge`** — an autonomous **long-form EDM build-and-drop tension engine** (a 6-state arc machine + look-ahead scheduler + sidechain ducking; generative-autonomous · CSS-compositor · neon-electric — Karel's explicit journey-engine-alternative directive + ambition #4 long-form, the jury's "nearly untouched" criterion, and a genuine pole rotation off the calm-ambient monoculture). (Freshness note: `BroadcastChannel` presence and the McAulay/Runions/EDM techniques are foundational — allowed under the freshness rule — but the *2026 live-music-agent framing* is what newly makes the low-latency-networked-ensemble and the responsive-co-performer verbs obvious; the honest audit also confirmed multi-user is a *rested* axis, not a grep-0 first — `694-pulse-ring` already used `BroadcastChannel` for a shared rhythm clock, so consort is framed as #2+#5, NOT a faked #1.) **Curated to `11128-dropforge`** (biggest concept-vs-monoculture move + a direct answer to a standing Karel request the lab has never delivered); banked `11096-consort` (RESURRECT-FIRST as a DEEP, ideally with real WebRTC cross-machine) + `11112-abyssglow` (three.js venation garden, rides Karel's particle loves) to IDEAS §1117. *Deferred calendar note (still open):* Ars Electronica 2026 "Future Begins" (Linz, Sep 9–13) program worth a scan when it fully drops.

## 2026-08-13 · §1116 — GROW A CORTEX FROM SOUND: the Kohonen self-organizing map as a topographic timbre-instrument (a grep-0 first the lab explicitly banked for a DEEP cycle)

15-min dive (WebSearch, dates checked; ledger 1113 W · 1114 DEEP · 1115 WIDE → **1116 DEEP** by strict alternation, and the 2026-08-12 jury's provocation #2 stands: the next DEEP must claim a real **grep-0 #1**, not another dead-inventor name-drop). **The honest first was found by auditing the lab, not the web** — §1114 itself flagged it: `kohonen`/`best.?matching.?unit`/`u-matrix`/`self-organizing map` grep to **0 real implementations** across 7500+ prototypes (the 36 `self.?organiz` hits are all emergence *prose* — Kuramoto, turmites, Lenia — none a SOM), and IDEAS §1114 explicitly **banked "self-organizing maps (Kohonen SOM)" as a DEEP candidate**. So this cycle consumes that research-derived seed (**case c**) and re-anchors it in fresh neuroscience-of-perception framing. **Kohonen 1982** ("Self-organized formation of topologically correct feature maps," *Biological Cybernetics* 43) is the foundational unsupervised competitive-learning technique — a 2-D grid of neurons that, fed high-dimensional inputs, self-organizes so neighbouring neurons come to represent similar inputs, i.e. it *grows a topographically-correct map of its input space with no labels and no supervision*. **Why it's on-theme (Karel's explicit "neuroscience of perception" ask):** the SOM is literally the standard model of how the cortex forms its sensory maps — the auditory cortex's **tonotopy** ("neurons arranged by the frequency they process, like the keys on a piano" — a resonance for a pianist), retinotopy, ocular-dominance columns. Live research shows the same principle is still the frame for cortical-map formation: *"Cortical topographic motifs emerge in a self-organized map of object space"* ([Science Advances 2023, sciadv.ade8187](https://www.science.org/doi/10.1126/sciadv.ade8187)) and end-to-end **topographic** deep nets as models of cortical map formation ([arXiv:2308.09431](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2308.09431)); the technique is also under active extension (Variational Quantum SOM, [arXiv:2504.03584](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2504.03584), 2025). **The portable idea:** feed a SOM a corpus of *timbres* (12-band spectral vectors) and it self-organizes into a **playable topographic timbre-terrain** where geography = sound and neighbours sound alike — you *watch a cortex tune itself to sound and then play the map it grew*. **Chains directly → today's DEEP `11048-cortexbloom` (case c).** DEEP raced ONE concept — *watch your music grow a cortex* — across three renderings of the SAME faithful Kohonen engine (BMU → full-grid Gaussian-neighbourhood update → exp-decaying α/σ): winner **`11048-cortexbloom`** (three.js height-field terrain whose vertices ARE the neurons — U-matrix→height buckles the sheet into gyri, centroid→teal/violet color, click a fold to play its timbre), over banked **`11064-tonotopy`** (Canvas2D flat topographic sheet — the most *legible* read: U-matrix ridges + a piano/frequency reference strip naming the emergent low→high gradient) + **`11080-somdrift`** (pure-CSS-compositor long-form — a plasticity floor + a drifting timbre-diet so the map *never settles*, different at minute 5 than minute 1). Off every live jury ban + my §1116 diversity audit (three.js scene-graph geometry — NOT the banned WebGL2 fragment-quad, and off the recently 4×-saturated SVG-DOM output · SOM competitive learning — NOT the banned PDE/CA/mass-spring/mechanism physics-sim family · seeded-corpus generative input — off mic/tilt/pointer · neural teal↔violet "living-cortex" palette — off warm-molten and off the 5×-saturated cosmic-ambient pole). Ambition honest **4/5** (#1 SOM/Kohonen grep-0 FIRST · #2 ≥3 subsystems: procedural timbre corpus + Kohonen engine + U-matrix/centroid readouts + three.js terrain + additive resynthesizer · #3 Kohonen-1982 *living technique ported*, not a dead name-drop · #5 this dive). **Verification lesson re-earned this cycle:** the *loser* `11064-tonotopy` self-reported "tsc 0 / eslint 0" but the authoritative `npm run build` (project strict tsconfig) caught a real `'ctx' is possibly null` at page.tsx:198 — a builder's own typecheck is NOT the authoritative gate; only `npm run build` is. The winner was re-built winner-only after removing both losers.

## 2026-08-13 · §1114 — A MIND AT THE EDGE OF CHAOS: reservoir computing / echo-state networks as a living, self-dreaming instrument that never repeats

15-min dive (WebSearch, dates checked; ledger 1110 W · 1111 W · 1112 D · 1113 W → **1114 DEEP** by strict alternation, and the 2026-08-12 jury's provocation #2 explicitly ordered the next DEEP to claim a **genuine grep-0 #1** instead of another dead-inventor name-drop or safe simulation). **FRESH, DATED ANCHOR — the 2026 edge-of-chaos reservoir-computing frontier.** WebSearch surfaced a live cluster: **[arXiv:2605.26848](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26848) "Design principles for optoelectronic light-scattering reservoir computing at the edge of chaos" (May 2026)** — which maps the reservoir's behavior onto **three physical control axes: reservoir dynamics, input–reservoir coupling, and interconnectivity**, and shows the useful regime sits at a three-axis *edge-of-chaos* optimum — alongside **all-optical ESN reservoir computing** ([arXiv:2504.08224](https://arxiv.org/html/2504.08224), Jan 2026) and **spin-wave edge-of-chaos physical reservoirs** (Apr 2026). The *portable idea* (not the hardware): **an echo-state network (Jaeger 2001) is a fixed random recurrent net you never train** — you read expressive linear projections off its high-dimensional transient state — and its defining **fading-memory / echo-state property** means it holds a decaying, nonlinearly-mixed trace of recent input and, tuned near spectral radius ρ≈1, produces dynamics that **never exactly repeat**. That is a perfect, on-theme altered-states instrument: a little mind you perturb that *rings, remembers, and improvises back*, with genuine internal STATE (different at minute 5 than minute 1). **Why it's the honest ambition move for THIS lab:** grepped `reservoir comput`/`echo state`/`esn`/`kohonen`/`self-organizing` across 7500+ prototypes → **0** for reservoir computing (a real criterion-#1 the jury kept demanding), and the three-axis edge-of-chaos framing hands the interaction design its three sliders for free. (Freshness note: ESNs are foundational — allowed under the freshness rule for stable techniques — but the *edge-of-chaos-as-a-playable-knob* framing is exactly what the current 2026 physical-reservoir wave newly makes obvious.) **Chains directly → today's DEEP `10984-echofold` (case a).** DEEP raced ONE concept — *a mind that dreams your phrase back, drifting* — across three renderings of the same real ESN engine: the winner **`10984-echofold`** (SVG-DOM call-and-response memory loom, the jury-praised rested substrate), over banked **`10952-reverie`** (Canvas2D luminous neural-constellation — you SEE the network and its pulses) + **`10968-driftmind`** (three.js phase-portrait field — the high-dim state as a wandering attractor), both built demoable + clean, IDEAS §1114. (Verification lesson this cycle: three "grep-0 first" candidates I liked — Ikeda test-pattern `1210`/`9720`, wave-function-collapse `353-collapse-score`, and the Hat/Spectre einstein monotile `7480-einstein` — were **already built**; at 7500+ prototypes every clean algorithmic first must be grepped against the codebase, not assumed. Reservoir computing and self-organizing maps both survived the audit; SOM is banked for a future cycle.)

## 2026-08-13 · §1113 — SINUSOIDAL RESYNTHESIS is having a differentiable moment: decompose any sound into gliding partials you can SEE and PULL OUT

15-min dive (WebSearch, dates checked). The active 2026 thread on `arxiv.org/list/eess.AS/recent` is **differentiable / GPU-accelerated additive & modal resynthesis** — e.g. a Mar-2026 Pulse-Train-Resonator model (parameterized pulse trains through recursive Karplus–Strong resonators) and a JAX-based differentiable modal framework for strings/membranes/plates with gradient-based inverse modelling. What they share: the field is re-tooling the *classic* **sinusoidal model** (McAulay–Quatieri 1986 — analysis = FFT peak-pick → partial tracking across frames; synthesis = a matched oscillator bank; the lineage behind SPEAR / Loris / spectral-modeling-synthesis) into something *manipulable in real time*. That reframing is the hook: nobody in the lab has ever exposed a sound's individual **partials** as first-class, playable objects. **Could become a prototype that** decomposes a live source into its gliding overtone-threads on screen and lets you *mute/solo one partial* — because the picture is driven by an actual resynthesis bank, silencing a thread audibly subtracts that overtone. (Freshness note: MQ itself is foundational — allowed under the freshness rule for stable foundational techniques — but the *interactive-manipulable* framing is what the current differentiable-resynthesis wave newly makes obvious; grep-0 across 1062 prototypes: `sinusoidal model`/`partial track`/`mcaulay` = 0.) → **chained directly into today's WIDE flagship `10904-partialharp` (case a).** Two more thin/absent anchors surfaced for the sibling lanes: **Riemann-sphere / stereographic projection** as a music surface (grep-0 `riemann sphere`) → `10920-sphereharp`; and the standing **2026 deep-Pacific bioluminescence** record (WHOI glowing-belly fish >7000 m, Mar 2026) → the verdant `10936-glowreef` resurrect. Also noted for the calendar: **Ars Electronica 2026 "Future Begins"** (Linz, Sep 9–13) with a dedicated *Sonic Saturday* at Anton Bruckner University — worth a scan when the program fully drops.

Karel's own §1111 MORNING note asked the same in different words: *"go DEEP on orbitroom, or keep going WIDE to drain the jazz/verdant/first-register backlog?"* — this cycle does both: a **DEEP that drains the jazz backlog** the jury has now named absent three verdicts running. **FRESH, DATED ANCHORS — the live-jazz-agent frontier of 2026.** WebSearch surfaced **LK_Jam ([arXiv:2606.21018](https://arxiv.org/html/2606.21018), June 2026) — a real-time human–AI interactive music-generation system built on a *Role-Aware GRU*** whose whole thesis is *turn-taking*: the machine must know whose turn it is and answer in role, at conversational latency. Alongside it, **["A Design Space for Live Music Agents"](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2602.05064) (Feb 2026)** and **BEAT ([arXiv:2604.19532](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2604.19532))** frame the field's core pain point precisely: existing music-transformer *tokenization requires a complete musical segment before it can generate*, which is fundamentally unsuited to real-time accompaniment where a response must form *as* the melody arrives — so the open problem is exactly **low-latency, role-aware turn-taking**, and the paper community names **jazz "trading fours" as the rigorous reference frame** for it. **Why this is the surprising hook for THIS lab (a portable *interaction model*, not a neural port):** you don't need a GRU in the browser to honor the finding — the transferable idea is the **role-aware call-and-response contract itself**, and the lab's own jazz lineage (`1812-sideman`) only ever *accompanies* — it has never built a partner that **takes a turn, banks your material, and DEVELOPS it across a whole form**, which is the jury's "nearly untouched" criterion #4 (long-form, stateful, memory/evolution). **Chains directly → today's DEEP `10872-choruskeeper` (+ 2 banked realizations):** a bebop duet with a persistent **motif library** — a 32-bar AABA in F loops for **3 choruses** with a real dramatic arc (chorus 1 STATE your fragments → chorus 2 the ghost DEVELOPS the motifs it banked, transposing/sequencing/inverting/augmenting them through the changes via Barry-Harris sixth-diminished voice-leading → chorus 3 TRADE & OUT, resolving to a held Fmaj7 — the tune **ends**, it does not loop), the banking and development drawn *legibly* on a Canvas2D FORM-MAP + motif-glyph panel so the memory is SEEN. Off every live jury ban (keyboard input — not mic/tilt · Canvas2D — not the banned WebGL2-quad or SVG-DOM · rule-based generative-jazz + motif-development — NOT a physics sim · midnight blue-note — not warm-molten, not violet-cosmic). Honest **3/5** with a genuine **#4** (the jury's exact demand): #2 (≥4 subsystems: phrase capture + persistent motif library + development ops + Barry-Harris changes/comp/walking-bass + form-map viz) + #3 (Barry Harris sixth-diminished, a *living pedagogy ported*, not a dead name-drop) + **#4 (long-form stateful arc that ends)**. Banked the other two realizations of the same concept: **`10856-tradefours`** (strict metered 4-bar trading over a ii–V–I, tightest classic read) + **`10888-echoduet`** (rubato/click-free call-and-echo with inversion/retrograde/augmentation + IOI phrase-end detection — the most distinct interaction model).

## 2026-08-12 · §1109 — THE GANZFELD AS A PORTABLE TECHNIQUE: Turrell's perceptual-dissolution light (a *living* 2026 install) ported into a screen-native long-form near-death descent

15-min dive (WebSearch, dates checked; ledger 1104 W · 1105 D · 1106 W · 1107 D · 1108 W → **1109 DEEP** by strict alternation, and the 2026-08-12 jury ordered the next DEEP to claim a real **#1 or #4** instead of another dead-inventor name-drop). **FRESH, DATED ANCHOR — James Turrell's *Ganzfeld* light works, with a current 2026 continuation.** Search surfaced **Turrell's *As Seen Below* (2026), a NEW permanent Ganzfeld/skyspace installation at [ARoS Aarhus Art Museum, Denmark](https://www.aros.dk/)**, plus Superblue Miami's current Ganzfeld room — a living, on-going body of work, not a historical footnote. The *portable technique* (a real perceptual method to port, not a name-drop): a Ganzfeld floods the entire visual field with a slowly-shifting uniform light so **"light, colour and space melt together, the eyes lose their frame of reference, and the boundaries of the room disappear"** across a 10–15 minute bath — engineered perceptual dissolution through luminance alone. That is directly implementable as a screen-native **whole-field luminance bloom** eased over seconds. **SAFETY translation (rule-9-adjacent, non-negotiable):** the physical installations use "periodic bursts of strobing light" — the build **explicitly REFUSES that**; every whole-field luminance change is smootherstep-eased + slew-limited to ≪ 3 Hz (slow drift, never a flash). **Why it's surprising / the honest ambition move for THIS lab:** I grepped every "grep-0 first" the jury named and NONE survives at cycle 1109 — PoseLandmarker body-tracking **44 files**, HRTF/PannerNode spatial audio **69 files**, WebRTC/multi-user **17 files**, MIDI-out **14 files**. The jury itself concedes #1 "is honestly claimed by essentially one piece." So the honest strong move is the OTHER criterion they praised `10184-ferrobloom` for claiming without inflation: a **genuine #4 multi-cycle commitment** on a massively-bigger concept, declining #1 outright. **Chains directly → today's DEEP build `10712-thresholdway` (+ 2 banked substrate-variants):** a long-form guided **near-death passage** you descend by holding a single "surrender" gesture — an endless tunnel toward a light with a Shepard–Risset *falling* endless glissando (`_shared/visionary/shepard.ts`, `dir:-1`), the words you type hanging in the tunnel as a re-readable transcript, and **5 phenomenological phases** (dissolution → the tunnel → approaching the light → **the clear-light ganzfeld bloom** → boundless void/return) so minute-5 ≠ minute-1 (the thin long-form-with-memory cell). The ganzfeld phase IS the Turrell port. Off every live jury ban (CSS-3D/SVG-DOM/three.js-geometry — not the banned WebGL2 fragment-quad; phased-descent + Shepard + luminance-bloom — not a physics sim; cosmic-void→white — not warm-molten; pointer+keyboard — not mic/tilt). Honest 4/5 (#2 ≥4 subsystems + #3 Turrell/Shepard living-port + #4 multi-cycle declared + #5 this dive), **NO false #1** (the near-death tunnel has lineage — §1108 banked `10680-thresholds`; the fresh move is SCOPE, the long-form phased arc + re-readable transcript).

## 2026-08-12 · §1108 — DATA AT THE THRESHOLD OF PERCEPTION: Ikeda's *test pattern* / *datamatics* as a portable technique (1-bit binary field + sine-and-click sonification), and three grep-0 registers the jury named absent

15-min dive (WebSearch, dates checked; ledger 1104 W · 1105 D · 1106 W · 1107 D → **1108 WIDE** by strict alternation, and the 2026-08-12 jury explicitly ordered a monoculture-break: ban the WebGL2-fragment-quad output, the physics-sim *family* (PDE/CA/mass-spring/mechanism) for two weeks, the warm-molten palette, and mic+tilt inputs — go WIDE with fresh tags). **FRESH ANCHOR — Ryoji Ikeda's *test pattern* / *datamatics* lineage, with a current 2026 continuation.** [ryojiikeda.com/project/testpattern](https://www.ryojiikeda.com/project/testpattern/) + [datamatics](https://www.ryojiikeda.com/project/datamatics/); the works archive lists a **2026 audiovisual installation *data.gram [nº11]*** (current, on-going). The *portable technique* (not a dead-inventor name-drop — a living artist's actual method): *test pattern* is "a system that **converts any type of data — text, sound, images, movies — into barcode patterns and binary patterns of 0s and 1s**," then plays them back at extreme frame-rates and **variable bit depths** to sit the image and its **sine-tone + click** sonification exactly at "the critical points of device performance and the **threshold of human perception**." That is a concrete, implementable algorithm: dither a data field to 1-bit, scan it, and let each lit bit ring a sine partial. **Why it's surprising for THIS lab / the ungrepped move:** the lab has 10,500+ prototypes but its palette has collapsed — the 2026-08-12 jury measured **warm-molten 6× in the last 15** and named **high-contrast Ikeda black-white-red as grep-0** (never shipped), alongside two other absent registers (verdant-bioluminescent; the near-death tunnel-to-light). A 1-bit datamatics field is also the strongest possible answer to the jury's "**rest the WebGL2 quad**" order — it wants SVG/DOM, not a shader. **Chains directly → today's WIDE build, 3 fresh-tag lanes each off every jury ban:** **Lane A `10664-testfield`** — pointer-drag scan-head across an SVG-DOM 1-bit data field, each lit bit a sine+click, Ikeda black/white/red (input: pointer · output: SVG-DOM · palette: Ikeda 1-bit — the grep-0 register, direct port of *test pattern*). **Lane B `10680-thresholds`** — type words to descend a **CSS-3D perspective** near-death tunnel-to-light toward a Turrell ganzfeld bloom (input: keyboard · output: CSS-3D · palette: cosmic-void→white — the tunnel register). **Lane C `10696-abyssbloom`** — camera-motion grows a **Vogel-phyllotaxis** (deterministic golden-angle lattice, explicitly NOT a physics sim) bioluminescent bloom in three.js instanced points (input: camera · output: three.js-points · palette: verdant-bioluminescent — the third absent register). Three unrelated inputs (pointer/keyboard/camera) × three thin outputs (SVG-DOM/CSS-3D/three.js-geometry, none a fragment-quad) × the three exact palettes the jury named absent — one fire, three directions out of the monoculture.

## 2026-08-12 · §1107 — PLAY LIGHT ITSELF: the color-music revival + continuous sustain-pedal DEPTH as a first-class musical gesture — a Thomas-Wilfred "Lumia" instrument where the pedal is the composition's memory

15-min dive (WebSearch + WebFetch, dates checked HONESTLY; ledger 1104 W · 1105 D · 1106 W → **1107 DEEP**). Two anchors, one fresh + one supporting. **(1) FRESH — "Color Songs: Musicians as Visual Artists"** ([thevisualist.org](https://thevisualist.org/2026/07/color-songs-musicians-as-visual-artists/), **July 2026, ~5 weeks old**) — a current show/survey of the *color-music* revival: musicians treating light and color as a composed medium in its own right. This is the living continuation of exactly the tradition **Thomas Wilfred** founded a century ago with his **Clavilux** organ and the art form he named **"Lumia"** (form · colour · motion as the three composable factors — see Dave Payling's *"Lumia and Visual Music"* framework, eContact! 19.2). **(2) SUPPORTING (design mechanic) — "High-Resolution Piano Sustain-Pedal DEPTH Estimation"** ([arXiv:2510.03750](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.03750)) — argues the sustain pedal is not a binary on/off but a **continuous-valued gesture** (partial pedaling, half-damping) that carries real expressive intent, and evaluates estimating its *depth*. **Honesty note (freshness mandate):** the obvious MIDI-expression paper MIDI-VALLE `2507.08530` is dated **July 2025** (>1 yr — rejected as stale); the real-time human-AI co-performance paper `2604.07612` is April 2026 (~4 mo, off the 30-day floor). So the genuinely-fresh anchor here is the color-music show, with the pedal-depth paper as a supporting design reference. **Why it's surprising for THIS lab / the ungrepped move:** the lab has color-organs in its deep history (`1107-chromatic-organ`, LOVED `1326-voice-cathedral`, banked `10584-clavilux`) but has **never made the SUSTAIN PEDAL the load-bearing compositional axis** — continuous pedal depth as a *memory* control that FREEZES played light into persistent standing "super-forms" that accumulate and slowly recompose. That turns a color-organ into a **long-form** instrument (minute-5 ≠ minute-1 — the thin long-form/memory menu-cell), and it is the **warmth-of-feeling** the 2026-08-11 jury demanded via a jewel/stained-glass palette that is off BOTH the diversity-banned warm-molten pole AND the jury-banned cold-void/clinical pole. **Chains directly → today's DEEP build (case a):** "LUMIA — play light itself," a MIDI-driven Wilfred color-organ (12-TET, NO just-intonation — cashing jury #2) whose pedal-depth accumulates a living stained-glass cathedral of your performance, raced across 3 renderers (CSS-compositor / SVG-DOM / WebGL2 → curate the freshest-output, muted-legible one). MIDI is the input axis furthest from the 3×-saturated body sensors (camera/mic/tilt) and 0× in the last 10 ships.

## 2026-08-12 · §1104 — FORMS (Playmodes): the graphic score as a two-way spectral instrument — and its INVERSE (sound → living notation) the lab has never built

Anchor: **FORMS**, a "generative visual-music scoring bot" by **Santi Vilanova / Eloi Maduell (Playmodes, Barcelona)** — [display.org.es/en/work/forms](https://display.org.es/en/work/forms), a networked ensemble exhibited after 2,000+ hours performing live on Twitch. Mechanism: probability/chance rules compose geometric shapes into a **graphic score**, then a **spectral sonification** reads the image column-by-column (y-position → frequency, brightness → amplitude — the ANS-synthesizer / Xenakis-UPIC lineage), explicitly framed in the Cage / Xenakis / Ligeti / Feldman graphic-notation tradition + the procedural line of Sol LeWitt & **Vera Molnár**. Why it's surprising for THIS lab: it makes the graphic-score a *first-class real-time medium*, not a still image — but it runs **score → sound**, the SAME direction as the lab's existing loved paint→sound pieces (`223-fourier-paint`, `243-spectral-cloud`). **The fresh, ungrepped move is the INVERSE:** feed in **real recorded audio** (a dropped file — or Karel's actual Path piano) and let its onsets/pitch/spectral-flux **auto-compose a living graphic score in the Cage/Feldman/Xenakis notation idiom** (Feldman grid boxes, Xenakis glissando sheaves, Cardew *Treatise* lines) that scrolls and is **re-performable** — scrub it and it re-sounds. That is sound → abstracted-notation (not a literal spectrogram — `223` already owns paint→spectrum), cashing the standing "use Karel's real music as the input" directive + his loves of `163-paths-visualizer`/`227-paths-granular`/`223-fourier-paint`. ## 2026-08-12 · §1103 — the granular density threshold: WHERE discrete rhythm becomes texture, and WHY a machine's tick-rate is a musical control

15-min dive (WebSearch, dates checked). The fresh anchor isn't a newer shader — the WebGPU/WebGL audio-reactive space this quarter is still the same glowing-particle-field idiom the jury just banned — but a **theoretical spine** freshly restated by 2026 generative-sonification work ([Leonardo/MIT Press 2026](https://doi.org/10.1162/LEON.a.2583), agent-based + granular; and the granular-synthesis primers restating Roads/Xenakis: *"sparse sonic grains are perceived as discrete, RHYTHMIC events; as grain density increases a perceptual continuity emerges that can be experienced as pitch/texture"*). That threshold — the point at which countable individual onsets fuse into a continuous roll/shimmer — is a **musical control axis the lab has never made playable directly**. Every mechanical rhythm machine has it built in: a slow escapement is spacious countable tick-tock; a fast one buzzes; a slow gear train ticks, a fast one whirs. **Could become a prototype that:** makes a *visible mechanism* whose drive SPEED sweeps the whole granular continuum — wind it slow and you hear/see separable interlocking strikes (rhythm-first, NO pitched drone — the jury's most specific unmet #2 ask), wind it fast and the same grains merge into mechanical texture. Chains directly into today's build (case a).


## 2026-08-12 · §1102 — After five straight WARM fires, rotate the whole palette AND kill the pitched-drone reflex: three registers the lab has forgotten — *record/replay a gesture* (Golan Levin's Yellowtail), *organic growth toward a presence* (space-colonization), and *pure rhythm with no drone at all* (Euclidean/Bjorklund) — the WIDE answer to a lab that is now monochrome-warm the way it used to be monochrome-cold

**Source(s) (WebSearch, dates checked; ledger 1099 D · 1100 W · 1101 D → **1102 WIDE**).** The last five ships (`10120`/`10184`/`10216`/`10248`/`10312`) are all **warm** (ember/molten-metal/terracotta/marigold/copper) — warmth was the 2026-08-11 jury's #1 order and is now *thoroughly* answered, to the point that "warm palette" has itself crossed the diversity-audit's ≥4-in-10 threshold and is banned this fire (the 1101 STATE flagged exactly this: "if 1102's jury flags too warm, rotate"). Cold-void + clinical-white is *also* jury-banned. So both poles the lab camps on are closed, and the fire must find the **middle registers the jury explicitly named as missing — organic, ritual, playful** — while continuing to kill the just-intonation reflex (JI is load-bearing in 6 of the last 15) and rest Canvas2D. Live-web scan this quarter confirms the WebGPU/WebGL audio-reactive creative-coding space is still overwhelmingly the *same* glowing-particle-field idiom (Robert Borghesi's ASTRODITHER; slime-mold presets) — reinforcing that the fresh move is not a newer shader but a **different verb**: a piece that *remembers a gesture*, one that *grows*, one that is *only rhythm*.

**Why it's surprising / the gap it exposes.** Three specific, long-standing jury asks are all still unbuilt: **(#5, "rotting banked six cycles")** the *reproducibility* affordance — record a trajectory, replay the exact phrase it sang — which the lab keeps flagging and never ships; **(the "organic" register)** genuine biological growth with accumulating state (not a loop) — the lab has lattices and filaments but no piece that *grows toward you*; and **(#2, most specific unmet)** "rhythm-first with **no pitched drone at all**" — nearly every one of 1100+ prototypes leans on a sustained pitched drone, so a purely percussive polymeter engine is close to grep-0 as a *sonic posture*. Each maps to a real named reference: **Golan Levin's *Yellowtail* / Audiovisual Environment Suite (MIT, 2000)** — draw a gesture, it loops as animated sound; **Runions, Lane & Prusinkiewicz, "Modeling Trees with a Space Colonization Algorithm" (2007)** + Anders Hoff's differential-growth studies; **Godfried Toussaint, "The Euclidean Algorithm Generates Traditional Musical Rhythms" (2005)** + the Bjorklund algorithm. Every lane is inharmonic-or-percussive (off JI), WebGL2 (off Canvas2D), sensor-driven (tilt / camera-motion / mic-onset — off the keyboard/pointer ban), and its palette is neither warm-molten nor cold-void (nacreous aqua / verdant bioluminescent / bold high-contrast graphic).

**Could become a prototype that** (→ this cycle's WIDE fan, ship 1 / bank 2): **(a)** `10376-tideglass` — **tilt** to steer a luminous drop across an iridescent tide-pool that RECORDS your gesture and REPLAYS the exact phrase, letting you layer a canon of your own motion (the rotting reproducibility verb, finally built; Yellowtail); **(b)** `10392-mycelium` — a living **space-colonization** web GROWS across the screen toward your **camera**-detected movement, each new tip plucking an inharmonic filament tone, dense at minute 5 in a way it wasn't at minute 1 (the organic register; Runions 2007 / Anders Hoff); **(c)** `10408-polymeter` — clap a pulse into the **mic** and a wheel of interlocking **Euclidean** rhythms blooms, purely percussive with NO drone (the rhythm-first register; Toussaint 2005 / Bjorklund). Chain today's dive (both palette poles closed → the forgotten middle registers, each a different verb) → today's WIDE build is direct (case a).

## 2026-08-12 · §1101 — Rayleigh–Bénard convection / solar granulation: the one self-organizing pattern the lab has *never* simulated, and it is simultaneously cosmic (the Sun's surface) AND warm-domestic (a pot of miso, cooling lava) — the WebGPU-compute-as-STRUCTURE rotation the jury + Karel both asked for

**Source(s) (WebSearch, dates checked; ledger 1098 W · 1099 D · 1100 W → **1101 DEEP**).** Convection is *the* canonical "how heat organizes itself into structure": heat a thin fluid layer from below and above a critical Rayleigh number `Ra = gαΔTd³/(νκ)` buoyancy beats viscosity+diffusion and the layer spontaneously self-organizes into a **honeycomb of overturning cells** — hot fluid up in the bright cell centers, cool fluid sinking in the dark intergranular lanes. It is not a bloom; the *cell tessellation is the emergent structure*. **This is literally the texture of the Sun's visible surface** — solar granulation is convection in the outer layer of a star (**Stein & Nordlund, "Simulations of Solar Granulation I," ApJ 1998**) — and equally the texture of miso soup, cooling lava crust, and a heated frying pan (**Henri Bénard 1900; Lord Rayleigh, "On convection currents in a horizontal layer of fluid," Phil. Mag. 1916**). Fresh, live-web-practical confirmation this quarter: **mysimulator.uk's interactive browser Rayleigh–Bénard simulator (article updated June 2026)** and **FluidX3D's real-time 3D GPU Rayleigh–Bénard convection** (lattice-Boltzmann on the GPU) show it runs interactively in-browser now; and **ShaderVine (2026)** — a WebGPU shader/compute-sim editor "built for the agentic era," 16 built-in compute simulations (reaction-diffusion et al.) on a ping-pong storage-texture architecture, explicitly "architected to be maintained with Claude Code" — is proof the WebGPU-compute-ping-pong idiom is now standard tooling.

**Why it's surprising / the gap it exposes.** At 1100+ prototypes the lab has simulated DLA frost (`2028`), sandpile/SOC avalanches (`1470`, `2402`), the Ising critical point (`1145`), excitable-medium spiral sheets (`1231-cardium`), reaction-diffusion, ferrofluid Rosensweig (`10184`) — nearly every self-organizing system — **but never convection**, the most fundamental one of all and the only one that is *both* cosmic and warm-domestic. It cashes three standing orders at once: the jury's #3 ("make WebGPU do simulation-as-STRUCTURE, not another glowing particle field" — convection cells ARE structure), Karel's own 06:30 morning ask (rotate the substrate: four straight WebGL/three.js fires → a **WebGPU-compute** piece), and the jury's #1 ("bring warmth" — a molten copper/gold boiling surface). Sonically it is **inharmonic** (each cell's overturn rings a stretched metal chime; the convective roll is a warm drone) — off the JI reflex — and it has genuine **memory/evolution** (cells are born, grow, merge, and are subducted; minute-5 ≠ minute-1), the jury-praised nerve. #1 is NOT claimed (fluid sims exist in the lab); the value is FUSION/fresh-verb: convection-as-a-tilt-conducted-warm-instrument is grep-0.

**Could become a prototype that** (→ this cycle's DEEP fan, ship 1 / bank 2): **tilt a warm molten surface and conduct where it boils, as the self-organizing convection cells reorganize and sing their own overturning.** ONE north star raced via three genuinely different realizations: **(A)** `10312-emberflux` — a *true* 2D Boussinesq Rayleigh–Bénard thermal fluid on WebGPU compute (advect T+velocity, buoyancy, Jacobi Poisson projection), molten copper plumes; **(B)** `10328-solarskin` — a solar-*granulation* cellular tessellation (living Voronoi/centroidal froth, cells born→merge→subduct at the lanes) tuned to the photosphere, white-hot centers / dark lanes; **(C)** `10344-magmacrust` — convection UNDER a solidifying crust that cracks along the cell boundaries so molten light shows through the fractures (height-field + tension/fracture field). Chain today's dive (convection as the un-simulated self-organizing structure) → today's DEEP build is direct (case a).

## 2026-08-11 · §1100 — Warm process-art surfaces without a mic: three 19th-/20th-century analog processes (anthotype photograms, viscous singing-bowl flow, Eno/Radigue long-form drift) as sensor-driven warm AV — the WIDE answer to the jury's "bring warmth or die cold, and get off the mic-surface groove"

**Source(s) (WebSearch, dates checked; ledger 1098 W · 1099 D → **1100 WIDE**).** Three warm, organic process-families, each browser-feasible and each anchored on a *different* real sensor: **(1) Anthotypes / photograms** — a plant-pigment plate (marigold/turmeric/beet) where *light bleaches* the dye and shadows stay saturated, producing warm sepia/marigold monochromes (**Mary Somerville, 1842, first anthotypes; Anna Atkins' cyanotype photograms, 1843**); the modern web move is to drive the cumulative bleach from a live **camera** luminance buffer, so the world develops a warm photogram in ~30 s. **(2) Viscous gravity-driven thin-film flow** — a slow, cohesive high-viscosity fluid (honey/resin) that necks into strands and pools, driven by the device **tilt** (deviceorientation gravity), sonified as **singing-bowl inharmonic partials** where it pools (the bowl's clustered/stretched partial series is the antithesis of a just-intonation chord). **(3) Long-form generative drift** — a ~7-minute never-repeating warm sundown with genuine layer-memory state (**Brian Eno's "77 Million Paintings" generative ethos; Éliane Radigue's slow evolving long-form drone**), which fills the lab's starved "long-form with memory" cell.

**Why it's surprising / the gap it exposes.** The 2026-08-11 jury's #1 order was "bring warmth or die cold" (13/15 cold), plus "kill the just-intonation reflex," "rest Canvas2D," and "put a real sensor in the driver's seat." The last three fires answered warmth but all via a **mic → self-organizing surface** verb (1098 voice→ferrofluid, and 1099 hands→clay broke mic but stayed a deformation surface). This dive deliberately finds warm processes whose **primary sensor is NOT the mic** — camera-develop and tilt-flow — and one that is long-form-with-memory rather than an instantaneous surface, so the WIDE fan spreads across three genuinely different input × technique × palette cells (camera·bleach-diffusion·marigold / tilt·viscous-flow·honey-gold / long-form·state-walk·warm-sundown). All three use **inharmonic or drifting** sonic material, none uses Canvas2D (WebGL art layers), and none uses keyboard/pointer as primary input — clearing every jury ban simultaneously.

**Could become a prototype that** (→ this cycle's WIDE fan, ship 1 / bank 2): **(a)** `10248-sunprint` — point your **camera** at the world and a warm anthotype photogram slowly develops and sings as its edges resolve; **(b)** `10264-honeyfall` — **tilt** your phone to pour a slow river of warm honey that rings like a singing bowl where it pools; **(c)** `10280-slowsun` — a 6–8 minute never-repeating warm sundown with real layer-memory (Eno/Radigue), warmed by a tap-to-add-a-coal touch and optional room-breath mic. Chain today's dive (warm non-mic process surfaces) → today's WIDE build is direct (case a).

## 2026-08-11 · §1099 — Shape-matching soft bodies with a *plasticity threshold*: the meshless deformation model that makes virtual clay actually **remember** the hands that shaped it (the DEEP seed for a camera-kneaded clay lump, and the mic-rotation the last three fires forced)

**Source(s) (WebSearch, dates checked; ledger 1097 D · 1098 W → **1099 DEEP**).** Two solver families for real-time deformable "clay," both browser-feasible on a ~642-vertex mesh: **(1) mass-spring / Verlet** (Provot 1995 deformation constraints; Jakobsen 2001, *Advanced Character Physics*) — structural springs relax toward rest length + rest position, so the lump is **elastic** (dents *heal*); and **(2) region-based shape-matching** (**Müller, Heidelberger, Teschner & Gross, "Meshless Deformations Based on Shape Matching," SIGGRAPH 2005**) with a **plasticity threshold** — each overlapping cluster fits a best rotation `R` (polar decomposition) to its rest offsets and pulls particles to goals `gᵢ = R·qᵢ + cm`, and when a particle's strain exceeds a **yield** it permanently rewrites its rest offset, so the body is **plastic** (dents *stay* — clay memory). The 2026 confirmation that this is live-web practical: WilKam01's *GPU Soft Body Physics* bachelor thesis (2026) runs **XPBD + region-based shape-matching clusters** with tuned cluster spacing/radius/stiffness at interactive rates (**Macklin et al., XPBD, 2016** framing), and multiple WebGL/TypeScript PBD-from-scratch repos ship cloth+soft-body today.

**Why it's surprising / the gap it exposes.** The lab has *never* built a plastically-deforming soft body, and the two solvers give the same gesture two opposite souls — a lump that springs back vs. a lump that keeps every fingerprint — which is exactly a DEEP race (one concept, two genuinely different techniques). It also cashes three standing orders at once: it **rotates OFF mic** (1096/1097/1098 were all mic-driven — the flagged next-fire mandate), it puts a **real sensor in the driver's seat** (MediaPipe hands — the jury praised `9864-handroom` as the window's strongest build and noted sensors are only 4× of 15), and it is intrinsically **warm** (terracotta clay, ochre light) — the jury's #1 "bring warmth or die cold." Sonically it is **inharmonic** struck-bar + wet-clay granular squelch, not a just-intonation lattice — killing the JI reflex. Named refs anchor #3; four subsystems (MediaPipe hand-track + soft-body physics sim + strain-driven granular audio + 4-tier degrade ladder) anchor #2 → an honest 3/5.

**Could become a prototype that** (→ this cycle's DEEP build, `10200-handclay` elastic mass-spring **vs** `10216-claymemory` plastic shape-matching): **you reach your bare hands into the webcam and knead a glowing lump of warm clay.** An open palm dents it, a pinch grabs and stretches a peak. In the elastic build the clay slowly heals back to a sphere (living, breathing clay); in the plastic build every dent is permanent and the piece becomes a record of everything you did to it. MediaPipe HandLandmarker (loader copied verbatim from `9864-handroom`) with a 4-tier degrade to seeded ghost-hands that knead from frame one, so a muted phone sees clay alive on load. Deliberately camera-input (rests mic) and three.js imperative soft-body (rests the WebGPU-compute substrate the last two fires leaned on). Chain today's dive (plastic vs. elastic clay memory) → today's DEEP build is direct (case a); it also resurrects the ⭐⭐⭐ RESURRECT-FIRST §1098 `handclay` seed in a bigger DEEP frame. *(This dive deepened the §1098-banked MediaPipe-clay lane by adding the shape-matching/plasticity second solver — the "clay that remembers" half that the WIDE seed did not have.)*

## 2026-08-11 · §1098 — Ferrofluid & the Rosensweig (normal-field) instability: a self-organizing *hexagonal spike lattice* as a warm, metallic sound↔structure surface — the lab's own long-shelved ⭐⭐ seed, and a genuine 4/5 answer to the JURY's "make the GPU do simulation-as-STRUCTURE, not another glowing field"

Fluid Mech. 30, 1967**; the standing-wave / super-lattice regimes in cond-mat/0005224). The genuine **4/5** the jury demanded (provocation #4) is reached honestly via **#2 (≥3 subsystems) + #3 (Cowley–Rosensweig 1967 / Leitl) + #4 (explicit multi-cycle commitment — cycle-2: a second steerable magnetic pole + spatialized metal voices) + #5 (this dive)** — with #1 declined.

**Could become a prototype that** (→ this cycle's WIDE 4/5 lane, `10184-ferrobloom`): **your voice raises a living field of metal spikes.** A WebGL2 height-field evolves under a normal-field-instability rule (a preferred spatial wavelength excited above a mic-driven critical threshold, with capillary damping); RMS loudness → field strength (more/taller spikes), spectral centroid → spike sharpness/spacing, onsets → surface ripples; screen-space normals shade a warm liquid-metal surface with specular peaks + heat-glow. Audio is an inharmonic struck-metal partial bank + a low ferric drone + a "ting" on each spike birth. Deliberately WebGL2 (NOT WebGPU-compute — resting the substrate the last two fires leaned on). Lett. 69, 1629** — trees(fuel)→fire→ash on a lattice with ignition, spread-to-neighbor, and burnout, one of the canonical self-organized-critical systems.

## 2026-08-11 · §1096 — Voice → Chladni figure in real time: a plate that recognizes its own standing-wave patterns and closes the sound↔structure loop (the seed for the 4/5 lane, and a warm answer to the JURY's "kill the JI reflex, rest Canvas2D" order)

**Source.** Anon., *"ChladniSonify: A Visual-Acoustic Mapping Method for Chladni Patterns in New Media Art Creation"* (**arXiv:2605.09846, submitted 2026-05-11**). Generates training data from **Kirchhoff-Love thin-plate theory**, trains a lightweight CBAM-CNN to classify a live Chladni figure with 99.33% accuracy, and maps the recognized pattern back to its sine-wave driving frequency in real time (**<50 ms end-to-end**) — a controllable bidirectional sound↔pattern instrument for new-media art. (Foundational anchor: Ernst Chladni, *Entdeckungen über die Theorie des Klanges*, 1787 — sand on a bowed plate migrates off the antinodes and settles on the still **nodal lines**, drawing the mode's geometry.)

**Why it's surprising / useful.** The lab is technically fluent but (per the 2026-08-11 jury) cold, Canvas2D-bound, and reflexively tuned to just-intonation. Chladni is the antidote on every axis: the sonic material is a plate's **inharmonic** metallic ring, not a consonance lattice; the visual is **simulation-as-structure** (grains descending the |ψ| gradient onto nodal lines — emergent crystalline geometry, warm brass+sand), the one thing the jury explicitly asked WebGPU-compute to do instead of "another glowing particle field"; and voice is a **real sensor in the driver's seat**. ChladniSonify runs figure→frequency; the obvious inversion is **frequency→figure driven by your own voice** — hold a pitch, watch the sand crystallize into that tone's mode, higher pitch → finer figure.

**Freshness note (honest).** arXiv 2026-05-11 (~3 mo) — inside the freshness band for a new-media-art systems paper, and it is the direct, current causal reference for the build. Chladni/Kirchhoff-Love itself is foundational (tagged so). This is a case-(a) research→build cycle: the finding is implemented *this fire* as the `10088-resonograph` lane (the 4/5 swing: novel GPU nodal-settling technique + mic/pitch/WebGPU-compute/inharmonic-synth ≥3-subsystems + Chladni/ChladniSonify named reference + this dated dive).

**Could become a prototype that…** (a) BUILT THIS CYCLE — `10088-resonograph`: sing a pitch → tens of thousands of GPU grains settle onto the nodal lines of the nearest plate eigenmode, and the plate answers with its inharmonic modal voice. (b) BANKED — a **two-plate duet / cymatic call-and-response**: two players (or voice + a fixed drone) drive coupled plates whose figures interfere, so consonance/dissonance shows up as *geometric* beating between two sand fields (not a JI lattice — literal wave interference). (c) BANKED — **cymatic memory**: record a sung phrase as a trajectory through mode-space and replay it as a morphing sand animation (ties to the still-unbuilt §1074 "record-a-path-replay-a-phrase" affordance the jury keeps flagging).

## 2026-08-11 · §1095 — The luminosity threshold: the perceptual flip from "illuminated surface" to "self-luminous glow" (the causal spine of a Turrell Skyspace)

**Source(s).** Killian Duay & Takehiro Nagai, *"Optimal colors can predict luminosity thresholds in natural scenes"* (**PLOS ONE, published 2026-03-13**; preprint bioRxiv 2025.08.07.669217). The **luminosity threshold** is the luminance level at which a patch stops looking like *a lit surface* and starts looking *self-luminous* — a light source, a glowing volume. The paper shows the visual system judges this boundary against an **internalized reference of the optimal (physically-possible) surface colors** under the estimated illuminant: cross that gamut and the brain reclassifies the patch as *emitting* rather than *reflecting*, and — critically — observers land on the same threshold whether they report direct self-luminosity or merely a feeling of "unnaturalness."

**Why it's surprising / useful.** This is the *mechanism* of a James Turrell Skyspace, formalized. A Turrell aperture is tuned so the disc of real sky flips from "a hole to the sky" into "a solid, self-luminous plane you feel you could touch"; his light-chambers hold the field right at the surface↔emitter boundary so the air itself appears to become substance. The lab's banked `10024-lightnave` (breath-conducted volumetric light-nave) has the *look* but no engine sitting on this flip. Duay-Nagai give it a spine: drive the light-space's luminance across its own **luminosity threshold** and the shafts/aperture stop reading as "lit fog" and start reading as "glowing solid" — the felt payoff a breath or an upward gaze can push the field across. It also explains WHY the piece must be watched on a bright screen in a dim room: the threshold is set by the *ambient* estimate.

**Freshness note (honest).** PLOS ONE 2026-03-13 (~5 mo), a slow foundational perception area; this is its newest quantitative result on the surface/self-luminous boundary and it is the direct causal account for the build. Build is DEEP case (c) — resurrecting a banked seed — so the dated dive is the deliverable, chained to the build (each of the 3 approaches targets the luminosity-threshold flip as its payoff).

## 2026-08-11 · §1094 — Entoptic emergence: 1/f-noise stochastic resonance in the Ganzfeld + the phosphene / negative-afterimage seat

**Source(s).** Pistolas, Smets & Wagemans, *"Wave after wave: the suggestibility of noise in the experience of multisensory hallucinations under multimodal Ganzfeld stimulation"* (i-Perception, 2025; PMC12457770) — finds **1/f (pink) noise is near-optimal for inducing hallucinatory percepts** in a uniform Ganzfeld field, consistent with a **stochastic-resonance** account (a *suboptimal* noise level maximally facilitates spontaneous pattern formation in visual cortex), with emergent forms arriving in slow, highly *suggestible* waves. Corroborated on the endogenous side by current phosphene/entoptic work — TMS phosphene mapping (clinicaltrials NCT07110493, active into Dec 2026) and the "endogenous ocular luminescence" framework for phosphenes, retinal dark-noise and **negative afterimages** — i.e. the brain generating vivid light-percepts with no external light.

**Why it's surprising / useful.** The lab keeps *adding* stimulus to induce visionary states; this line says the opposite — a bath of structureless 1/f noise at a *tuned* level lets the viewer's own cortex do the hallucinating, and there is a stochastic-resonance **sweet spot** (too little = nothing, too much = washout). And the afterimage/phosphene results make the most literal "the screen isn't the image" trick real: prime the retina, blank it, and the viewer *renders the payoff themselves.*

**Freshness note (honest).** The Ganzfeld-SR paper is 2025 (~11 mo), not strict-30-day; perception-binding is a slow foundational area and this is its newest quantitative SR result, buttressed by Feb-2026 phosphene work. Tagged accordingly.

**Could become a prototype that…** (a) BUILT THIS CYCLE — `9992-afterimage` makes the delivered image the one the retina paints after the field blanks (entoptic, off the saturated void lane; case (a) research→build). ## 2026-08-11 · §1089 — the 2026 microtonal-controller frontier is a live protocol war (MPE per-note-bend vs MIDI 2.0 native microtuning), and the browser has no bridge to your rig — so the lab can be the first: an isomorphic-JI surface that plays OUT in exact tuning via MPE, grep-0

**Source (WebSearch, Aug 2026, dates checked; ledger 1086 W · 1087 D · 1088 W → **1089 DEEP**; governed by JURY.md 2026-08-10 provocation #3 "zero embodied-room / MIDI-OSC-out in 15 cycles" — the MIDI/OSC-out lane is the last of the three still uncashed, and #6 "extend, don't restart").** The dive maps the *current* microtonal-performance-controller landscape. **The Microtone Fox — "Setting up a microtonal MIDI controller: options and workflows" (Medium, May 2026)** + the 2026 MPE roundups (gearnews *Best MPE Controllers*, jamesm.blog) + the **Lumatone FAQ** document a live, unresolved debate about *how* microtonality should reach a synth. Two camps: **MPE per-note pitch-bend** — each note claims its own member channel so it can be bent to any exact non-12-TET frequency (the Expressive E **Osmose**, Dualo **Exquis**, Haken **Continuum** path); vs **MIDI 2.0's** forthcoming native microtuning — the Lumatone (280-key hex isomorphic board) is MIDI-2.0-ready and its makers explicitly say they "see no need for MPE." Browser-side microtonal synths exist (Frequency Explorer, Microtonal Fabric, Dynamic Tonality) but they are self-contained instruments — **none bridges the browser to your own DAW/hardware in exact tuning.**

**Why it's surprising / the gap it exposes.** Consonance-as-geometry and JI are alive in the lab (1087 purelock, the Tonnetz line), but the corpus is grep-0 on the one thing a working microtonalist actually needs: **MPE per-note-channel pitch-bend to an exact microtonal Hz, sent OUT.** `291-harmonograph` is the only prior MIDI-OUT and it sends plain 12-TET `[status,midi,vel]`; nothing round-robins member channels 2–16 with a per-note bend14. The jury named the MIDI/OSC-out lane a grep-0 category three times (STATE §1086/§1087); the ⭐⭐⭐ `9704-mpelace` / `9656-mpegrid` seeds have sat un-shipped waiting for exactly this slot. A DEEP that ships the bridge cashes the last uncashed jury-#3 lane AND carries a rare real ambition #1.

**Chain → today's build (research-first case c + a fresh dated anchor).** The §1089 dive (2026 MPE-vs-MIDI2.0 microtuning debate; no browser bridge) + the recent-research IDEAS seeds ⭐⭐⭐ `9704-mpelace` (§1087, itself Milne–Sethares–Plamondon-chained) / `9656-mpegrid` (§1086) → today's DEEP: ONE concept ("the first browser microtonal instrument that plays OUT via MPE and sounds unplugged, with consonance made visible") raced via two interaction approaches — **A `9768-mpelace`** (discrete isomorphic Wicki–Hayden/Bosanquet hex keyboard, 5-limit JI + 31-EDO toggle, per-note MPE bend-before-note-on, per-cell shimmer, neo-Riemannian PLR auto-arpeggiator — **SHIPPED**) and **B `9784-glideharp`** (continuous fretless pitch ribbon, *streamed* per-frame per-note bend, beating→Lissajous that locks-still on pure ratios — **banked IDEAS §1089**). **Could become a prototype that does X:** an OSC-out-over-WebSocket variant for TouchDesigner/Max routing, or a MIDI 2.0 UMP path once browsers expose it — the same surface speaking whichever protocol the rig wants. Refs: Bosanquet · Wicki–Hayden · Lumatone/MIDI-2.0 · MPE spec (RP-053) · Milne–Sethares–Plamondon 2007 · Plomp & Levelt 1965 · Osmose/Continuum · Xen-wiki 31-EDO. Sources: medium.com/@themicrotonefox/setting-up-a-microtonal-midi-controller-options-and-workflows · lumatone.io/faq · gearnews.com/best-mpe-controllers · jamesm.blog/music-production/best-mpe-controllers-2026.

## 2026-08-10 · §1087 — you can make consonance *visible*: acoustic beating (Plomp–Levelt roughness) is minimised at exactly the small-integer just-intonation ratios, so a beating field that FREEZES to stillness at a pure ratio turns "in tune" into something you can see, not only hear — and nobody has sent microtonal MPE MIDI-OUT from the lab yet

**Source (WebSearch, Aug 2026, dates/lineage checked; ledger 1085 D · 1086 W → **1087 DEEP**; governed by JURY.md 2026-08-10 provocations #5 "ban pentatonic — force microtonal/just-intonation/spectral" + #6 "extend/deepen a claimed line").** Sweep on microtonal consonance, isomorphic tuning controllers, and psychoacoustic roughness. Named-reference spine: **Milne, Sethares & Plamondon 2007, "Isomorphic Controllers and Dynamic Tuning: Invariant Fingering over a Tuning Continuum"** (*Computer Music Journal* — the Wicki-Hayden/harmonic-table layout gives the SAME fingering across a whole tuning continuum, so you can glide 12-TET → just → 31-EDO without relearning the instrument); **Plomp & Levelt 1965, "Tonal Consonance and Critical Bandwidth"** (perceived roughness peaks when two partials sit ~¼ critical-band apart and VANISHES at small-integer frequency ratios — i.e. beating stills exactly at 3/2, 5/4, 7/4…); **Sethares, *Tuning, Timbre, Spectrum, Scale*** (dissonance curves derived from a timbre's partials); Helmholtz; Erv Wilson's lattices. 2026 microtonal-hardware context (a live crop of JI/EDO keyboards on the market) corroborates real musician appetite.

**Why it's surprising / the gap it exposes.** The lab has an analytical *living-tuning-lattice* line — `6728-commawalk` → `6808-spectrascale` → `7416-temperlattice` — that DERIVES a scale from a timbre's spectrum and watches the lattice re-crystallise. But two things are genuinely grep-0: (1) nothing makes **acoustic beating itself the visible medium** — a moiré/interference field whose spatial beat-rate is driven by the *audio* beat-rate between voices, so landing a pure ratio locks the image to STILLNESS and drifting off makes it throb (the closest prior, `8808-truelevel` "hold level for a pure tone", was a banked SEED, never shipped); and (2) nothing sends **MPE per-note-bend *microtonal* MIDI-OUT** — `291-harmonograph` is the only prior MIDI-out and it emits plain 12-TET `[status, midi, vel]` (grep-verified), so a real just-intonation performance surface that plays your DAW/hardware in exact ratios is unbuilt.

**Chain → today's build (research-first case c).** DEEP dive consuming two IDEAS seeds banked from recent research-driven fires — ⭐⭐⭐ `latticewalk` (IDEAS §1084: "walkable JI Euler-Tonnetz where beating physically vanishes at pure ratios + a just↔12-TET A/B toggle"; the jury independently re-flagged it "the strongest musician-facing seed in the queue") and ⭐⭐⭐ `mpegrid` (IDEAS §1086: "first WebMIDI-out / MPE-microtuning surface") — raced as ONE north star, **"consonance you can SEE,"** via two approaches: **A `9688-purelock`** (exploration/legibility — a walkable 7-limit JI lattice as a hand-rolled WebGL2 interference field that freezes at pure ratios, just↔12-TET A/B, Ikeda b/w/r, seeded auto-walker) and **B `9704-mpelace`** (performance-tool — an isomorphic Wicki-Hayden 5-limit-JI/31-EDO keyboard with per-cell beating-shimmer and the grep-0 MPE microtonal MIDI-OUT, clinical high-key, seeded auto-arp). **Could become a prototype that does X:** a *dissonance-curve* instrument in the Sethares sense — feed it any timbre (even Karel's piano samples) and it re-draws where the beating-nulls fall, so the "in-tune" positions MOVE with the sound; the visible-stillness field then teaches your ear where consonance lives for THAT timbre.

## 2026-08-10 · §1086 — the networked-music field's most interesting recent turn is to stop *fighting* latency and treat the delay between players AS the instrument — the exact reframe that lets a browser multi-user room (jury-flagged 0× for 15 cycles) become a distributed canon

**Source (WebSearch, Aug 2026, dates checked; ledger 1084 W · 1085 D → **1086 WIDE**; governed by JURY.md 2026-08-10 provocation #3 "zero embodied-room and zero multi-user in 15 cycles").** Sweep on real-time collaborative browser music + networked-ensemble latency, current work. Two load-bearing findings: (1) **NIME 2025, "Exploiting Latency in the Design of a Networked Music Instrument"** (nime.org/proceedings/2025/nime2025_69.pdf) — the native transmission delay of a networked instrument is used *as a musical device* (delay/reverb/feedback-delay embedded on the bidirectional path), i.e. latency reframed from bug to compositional material. (2) The **local-synthesis pattern** (Cornelljam-style sub-50 ms LAN ensembles): don't stream audio — transmit only control/timing events and have each client synthesize locally, so the room scales at zero bandwidth. Sync stays musical via a **shared quantization grid / Global Metronome** (Oda & Fiebrink, NIME 2016) — everyone locked to one temporal reference so packet jitter/clock-drift never smears the beat.

**Why it's surprising / the gap it exposes:** the lab has shipped 1000+ prototypes and **multi-user/WebRTC is grep-0** — every piece is one screen, one player (the jury names it: "zero multi-user in 15 cycles," provocation #3, alongside two other 0× lanes it lists in the same breath — depth-camera/projection spatial, and MIDI/OSC-out). The latency-as-instrument reframe is what makes multi-user *interesting* rather than just "the same synth, networked": the delay between players becomes an audible, tunable canon offset you compose with. And BroadcastChannel gives a genuinely multi-participant transport (two tabs really do sync) with **no signaling server and no API route** — so the mechanic is demoable today, with seeded synthetic peers standing in for a muted 06:30 phone. **Could become prototypes that:** (A) a shared room where several tabs/people weave one piece and the inter-peer latency IS the instrument → a distributed canon on a clinical network-diagram (`9640-latencyloom`, the research-chained headliner); (B) a WebMIDI-out microtonal surface — an isomorphic JI/31-EDO lattice sending real per-note MPE pitch-bend to your DAW, the MIDI/OSC-out lane (`9656-mpegrid`); (C) the screen as a one-point-perspective ROOM you walk through, camera-silhouette depth → HRTF spatial audio, the depth/projection lane (`9672-shadowroom`). All three chained into this WIDE fan-out — one fire, all three of the jury's named 0× categories at once.

## 2026-08-10 · §1085 — distributed memory horizons: a machine can carry a THEME over minutes if a long history window sits above the local scales (DSMR, Feb 2026) — the exact upgrade mnemonic's flat 10-motif bank was missing

**Source:** *Depth-Structured Music Recurrence: Budgeted Recurrent Attention for Full-Piece Symbolic Music Modeling* (DSMR), **arXiv:2602.19816, submitted Feb 23 2026** (date verified via abstract fetch). Corroborating: *A Design Space for Live Music Agents* (CHI 2026, dl.acm.org/doi/10.1145/3772318.3791291 — a taxonomy of interactive/co-improvising music agents and their memory/listening dimensions); Theme Transformer (arXiv:2111.04093, [older, foundational] — contrastive clustering of recurring 2-bar segments into a conditioning *theme* rather than local continuation).

**What it is / why it's surprising.** DSMR trains end-to-end on *complete* compositions by **distributing memory horizons across network layers**: lower layers get **extended history windows** that capture "motif repetition and developmental variation," while upper layers keep **short, uniform windows** for local detail — a cheap way to hold long-range structure that most models lose. The surprise for this lab: it names, at the architecture level, exactly the gap in our own richest build. `9080-mnemonic` already runs a **two**-time-scale listener (fine per-frame pitch/onset + coarse phrase segmenter) but its MEMORY is FLAT — a 10-slot motif bank, every quote a one-off echo; minute five sounds like minute one. DSMR says: add a THIRD, LONG horizon on top and let it carry the developmental variation.

**Chain → today's build (case a).** Today's dive → today's DEEP: extend mnemonic into its claimed **cycle 2** by bolting a long-horizon THEME memory onto the existing short scales, so recurring motifs reinforce a lasting theme developed over minutes.
