## 2026-08-10 · §1084 — the two idioms the lab has NEVER entered are Ikeda's monochrome-data *datamatics* and just-intonation/spectral tuning; today's jury verdict makes both the mandated escape from a warm-glow + pentatonic monoculture

**Source (WebSearch, Aug 2026, dates checked; ledger 1082 W · 1083 D → **1084 WIDE**; governed by JURY.md 2026-08-10 "break the monoculture").** Sweep on the Ryoji Ikeda *datamatics* lineage + microtonal/just-intonation browser audio. Ikeda's **`data.gram [nº11]` (2026)** continues *datamatics* (series b.2006, incl. *data.tron*, *test pattern*, *the critical paths* 2024): masses of data rendered as **black/white monochrome grids, streaming code, scan-lines, and pure sine/impulse test-tones** — a severe, clinical idiom that is the categorical opposite of the lab's warm-amber and cosmic-indigo glow-fields. Separately, just-intonation/microtonal tuning (Euler *Tonnetz*, Partch, Johnston) is the tuning tradition where whole-number ratios (3/2, 5/4) let intervals *lock without beating* — the direct antithesis of the near-universal major/minor-pentatonic the jury just soft-banned. Ikeda official: https://www.ryojiikeda.com/project/datamatics/ · Tonnetz/just-intonation: standard theory.

**Why it's surprising / the gap it exposes:** grep-0 in the lab on BOTH axes. (a) *Rendering language:* every prior piece is glow / bloom / organic / particle — the lab has **never** rendered in the Ikeda monochrome-data idiom (precise b/w/r scan-fields, numerals, tick columns; sound TRIGGERED by a scan line crossing data). (b) *Tuning:* pentatonic is in pendulums, billow, tensegrity, morphochoir, formcanon, passage, handflux… the lab has **never** built on the harmonic series or a just-intonation lattice where the *beating itself* is the subject. Today's jury verdict independently arrives at the same two escapes (provocations #1 "Ikeda black-white-red" + #5 "ban pentatonic → just-intonation/spectral") plus #4 ("nothing conceptual/critical has EVER shipped"). **Could become prototypes that:** (A) a self-scanning *datamatics* field where the scan line's crossing of a lit data-column triggers a harmonic-series test-tone → `9576-datascan`; (B) a piece that permanently erases itself as it plays (Basinski's self-destroying tape as the conceptual/critical lane) → `9592-disintegration`; (C) a walkable just-intonation *Tonnetz* where beating vanishes at pure ratios and a just↔12-TET toggle A/Bs the roughness → `9608-latticewalk`. All three chained straight into this cycle's WIDE fan-out — one fire, three escapes from the monoculture.

## 2026-08-09 · §1076 — The 2026 low-latency neural-synth frontier keeps proving the SAME thing: you don't need the neural net to get timbre transfer — the DDSP *decoder* (a harmonic-oscillator bank + filtered noise driven by live pitch+loudness) is the load-bearing part, and it's small enough to run as plain Web Audio in a browser.

Adjacent fresh AV signal from the same dive: WebGPU-compute is the community's current creative-coding substrate of choice (ShaderVine's 16 built-in compute sims; "Beyond WebGL: Real-Time Fluid Simulations Using WebGPU Compute Shaders", May 2026) — the jury's flagged-thin substrate is where the field is actually shipping.

It's also the cleanest way off my emerging inline-SVG-notation rut and onto the jury-thin WebGPU substrate.

**Could become a prototype that** (→ this cycle's WIDE lane A): a real-time **DDSP-style harmonic-plus-noise resynthesizer** — mic → autocorrelation pitch + RMS loudness → drive ~48–64 harmonic sine partials (per-voice spectral-envelope presets) + a loudness-tracked filtered-noise band → you hear your voice re-voiced while your melody survives, and the live harmonic stack IS the WebGPU visual. Non-physics (obeys the sim-instrument ban), off every banned substrate, mic input (off the jury-banned pointer). Named refs: Engel et al. DDSP (ICLR 2020) + arXiv:2503.11562. ## 2026-08-09 · §1073 — The Aug-2026 listening frontier says the missing axis is TIME SCALE: musical properties decode at hierarchy levels that track their temporal scale (phrase boundaries = coarse, harmonic detail = fine). 9016 only listens at the FINE scale — the DEEP is to add the COARSE one (motif memory + long-form development).

**Source (WebSearch on real-time music co-creation / machine-listening, Aug 2026, dates checked; ledger 1070 W · 1071 D · 1072 W → **1073 DEEP**).** The current thread past last cycle's *listen→respond* taxonomy (arXiv:2602.05064) is **hierarchical machine listening**: **"Helping Music Co-Creation Agents ‘Listen’ Well: Hierarchical Self-Supervised World Models" (arXiv:2608.04378, Aug 2026)** trains a JEPA-style world model on MIDI piano-roll and finds that **the level at which a musical property becomes decodable tracks its musical time scale** — phrase boundaries read off the coarsest levels, note density and harmonic detail off the finest. Adjacent fresh work: Co-policy (arXiv:2606.19914, responsive human–AI co-creation), latent-diffusion accompaniment in MAX/MSP (arXiv:2604.07612), RIME (arXiv:2607.19605, motif/pattern-aware post-production).

**Why it’s surprising / the gap it exposes:** yesterday’s `9016-shadowhand` is a genuine listening accompanist — but it listens at **one** time scale, the instantaneous frame (onset/chroma/energy → harmonize the CURRENT note, no memory). 2608.04378 says the whole payoff is **multi-scale**: to be a real partner an agent must also hear at the COARSE scale — capture phrase-level MOTIFS, hold them in memory, and DEVELOP them over minutes. The lab has ~a dozen reactive duet/answer pieces (`251-live-duet-trader`, `1366-answer-piano`, `1812-sideman`, `8440-duetmind`) but **none with long-form motivic memory** — they all answer the *last* phrase, none remember and vary your themes across a 4–8-min arc.

**Could become a prototype that** (→ this cycle’s DEEP): extends 9016 into a **two-time-scale accompanist** — FINE (the existing frame-wise shadow harmony) PLUS COARSE (a phrase-segmenter builds a live MOTIF BANK from Karel’s real Path piano / a dropped file / a mic, and over the arc the agent stops merely harmonizing-below and starts **stating your captured motifs back, transformed** — invert / transpose / augment — so minute 4 sounds *different* from minute 1). **Honest chain:** today’s dive (multi-scale listening) → today’s build (add the coarse scale to 9016). Named refs: Krumhansl–Schmuckler key-finding (1990), George Lewis *Voyager*, Robert Rowe *Machine Musicianship* (Cypher). **Claims criterion #4 (multi-cycle)** openly — the jury’s never-claimed ask.

Sources: [Helping Music Co-Creation Agents ‘Listen’ Well (arXiv:2608.04378)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04378); [Co-policy (arXiv:2606.19914)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19914); [Real-Time Human–AI Musical Co-Performance / latent diffusion + MAX/MSP (arXiv:2604.07612)](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2604.07612); Krumhansl & Schmuckler key-finding (1990); Robert Rowe, *Machine Musicianship*.

## 2026-08-09 · §1072 — The 2026 wave is data→AI-music GENERATORS; the un-built inversion is an agent that LISTENS to a real human performance and answers it — realized on Karel's real Path piano.

**Source (WebSearch on real-time data-sonification / generative-music / live-music-agents, 2026; ledger 1069 D · 1070 W · 1071 D → **1072 WIDE**; driven by the fresh 2026-08-09 Concept Jury's five-tag ban + "core technique NOT a physics simulation").** The 2026 browser thread is dominated by data→music GENERATION: platforms mapping data to real-time AI music via **Google Lyria / ElevenLabs** with synchronized visuals; **AEROSONIC** (unsupervised weather→MIDI via Isolation Forest); geographic land-cover → ambient soundscapes (Mapbox + Web Audio); "Claude Code session as ambient music." The one that reframes the build: **"A Design Space for Live Music Agents" (arXiv:2602.05064, 2026)** — a taxonomy of *listen → infer → respond* musical agents (as opposed to autonomous generators). 

**Why it's surprising / the gap it exposes:** the lab has hundreds of GENERATORS and VISUALIZERS but — checked by grep — **zero** pieces where an agent listens to a *real human performance in real time and answers it musically*. And the same dive KILLED the obvious real-data lanes: **live-earthquake sonification is already built** (`1193-tremor-core` pulls the exact USGS all-day GeoJSON + `4856-orrery/streams.ts`) and **live-Wikipedia-edit sonification is already built** (`437-wiki-pulse` stream.ts+audio.ts, `1024-wiki-bells`) — so the real-data FAMILY is retired as a fresh-lane source (consistent with §1071's "real-world data saturated"). The un-built, jury-aligned move was therefore a **score-following accompanist** — non-physics (jury #1), on the thin WebGPU-compute substrate (jury #4) — and it is the natural vehicle to finally deliver **Karel's REAL Path recording** (jury #3, ~37 cycles with zero true delivery).

**Could become a prototype that:** fetches Karel's actual `/api/audio/549fc519…` recording, score-follows it live (spectral-flux onset + 12-bin chroma/key + IOI-tempo), and plays a generative complementary FM voice that harmonizes below his line and rests when he's dense — a "shadow hand" on the keyboard, visualized as two WebGPU-compute particle flows (→ built this cycle as the WIDE winner `9016-shadowhand`; named refs Barry Vercoe *The Synthetic Performer* ICMC 1984, Roger Dannenberg *On-Line Algorithm for Real-Time Accompaniment* ICMC 1984). **Honest chain:** today's dive → today's build (the winner). **Still-fresh for future fires:** a score-*aligned* (not feature-based) follower that anticipates and recovers after a jump; accompanying ANY dropped file or a live mic performer; the AI-pipeline GENERATE direction (Lyria/ElevenLabs) remains the standing build-or-strike (needs `FAL_KEY`).

Sources: [A Design Space for Live Music Agents (arXiv:2602.05064)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.05064); Barry Vercoe, *The Synthetic Performer in the Context of Live Performance* (ICMC 1984); Roger B. Dannenberg, *An On-Line Algorithm for Real-Time Accompaniment* (ICMC 1984); the 2026 browser data-sonification wave (Lyria/ElevenLabs, AEROSONIC).
